# Card rulebook review — 2026-10-27

Review of all 183 cards on the live `新卡组卡` tab (gid 648135790) against their
implementations and comments. Worktree `.claude/worktrees/card-rulebook-review`,
branch `worktree-card-rulebook-review`.

## Method

- The live sheet was fetched on 2026-10-27 and compared cell-for-cell with
  `docs/rulebook/cards-sheet.csv`: **identical** — the review is against current text.
- Every card was read in full beside its cell, clause by clause: header quote fidelity,
  implementing code and its `// 规则书:` citation, `TODO(规则书)` presence/accuracy, and behaviour
  (target, amount, dice, timing, order, condition, optionality, caps, owner vs user, extra effects).
- Findings were independently re-read by an adversarial verifier that could reject them; two
  further passes per column (one in each shard, one in the full run) swept for missed defects
  and were verified the same way.
- Probe tests named `zz_*` in the evidence text were scratch files under `rules/tests/tests/`
  and are not committed, and the temporary `DBG` instrumentation they keyed on was reverted.
- `tools/rulebook/check.py` was run first: all 183 cards already carry verbatim headers and
  `// 规则书:` citations (0 card problems). The comment findings below are therefore call-site
  citation, TODO and wording defects, not missing headers. (Unrelated baseline: 28 event rules
  lack `CardMeta::text`; events are outside this sheet.)

**This is a review, not a fix.** No rule body was changed. `[recorded]` means an accurate
`TODO(规则书)` and/or BACKLOG/TEST-FINDINGS entry already records the gap; everything else is new.

## Summary

- **1733** verified findings on **180** of **183** cards.
- Severity: **125 high**, **587 medium**, **1021 low**.
- `[recorded]` 275; new/unrecorded **1458** (all high are unrecorded).
- Clean cards (no finding): **3** — `PP:有你与我在这里共度`, `R:轨迹`, `通用:GREAT`
- By class: wrong_behavior 580, misleading_comment 362, missing_todo 259, missing_citation 241, missing_clause 119, extra_effect 78, wrong_quote 52, stale_todo 42

## High-severity defects

Each changes the rule outcome in normal play. Full text for every finding is in the JSON/CSV;
the lines below give clause, code, reason and a fix direction.

### `AG:即使夕阳落山` — missing_clause — `rules/cards/card-ag/src/sunset.rs:21`
- Sheet: 「[指定][使用者]拥有的四个有房子的格子和[使用者]以外的所有玩家。」
- Code: `]).meta(&card_sdk::meta::CardMeta::new()`
- Why: The body designates every other player via ctx::target_all, but the CardDef sets no prop::DESIGNATES. In game-rules wasm_rules::designations() returns an empty Vec unless the card carries that prop, and the prop is also what opens the Effect-on-DESIGNATES play timing (CROSS-TESTS C13). So 通用:网络链接异常, though offered on its Card kind, reads an empty designation list and falls into its clause 2 「没有[指定]目标则抵消其所有的效果」 (full cancel) instead of clause 1 (cancel one designation); AG:宣战布告 is likewise not offered at the play window. (Nuance on the original claim: declare_war is not "never offered at all" -- it can still fire at the later `pay` effect window raised by ctx::transfer, i.e. after the demolition instead of as part of the play's own designation timing.) The twin card 通用:登上武道馆 sets exactly this prop for the same clause.
- Fix: Add `.props(&[(card_sdk::abi::prop::DESIGNATES, 1)])` on the CardDef (as budokan does); the designation set is `world.others(user)`.

### `AG:回家的路上绕个道` — wrong_behavior — `rules/cards/card-ag/src/detour.rs:64` [recorded]
- Sheet: 「(1)此卡可作为反击使用」
- Code: `rules_status::move_blocked(player_id).is_none() && some_plan_fires(player_id)`
- Why: The clause is unreachable in the only window it can answer. `main_move` sets `self.w.turn.main_moved = true` (crates/game-core/src/engine/play.rs:1340) before raising `diceRollResult` (play.rs:1421), and `cant_move` returns 2 for `main_moved` (crates/game-rules/src/wasm_rules.rs:2142); `move_blocked` is that same call (rules/status/src/lib.rs:116-117). So the guard is a constant false in the window: the card can never be offered as a counter. Rebuilt card wasm and re-ran `zz_detour_probe`: `offered=false main_moved=true turn=0`; with only this check deleted, `offered=true`. The TODO above states the opposite premise (that only the status case is handled).
- Existing TODO: // TODO: Only handle the `blocked by status` case, not the `not_my_turn` and `already` case.
- Fix: Use `ctx::cant_move(player_id) != Some-the-status-3` instead (accept already-moved / not-your-turn) and keep only a status/jail refusal.

### `AG:回家的路上绕个道` — wrong_behavior — `rules/cards/card-ag/src/detour.rs:79`
- Sheet: 「并将下一次的移动掷骰变更为1d6」
- Code: `On::Action(Action::DiceRollAfter("slot('detour_next_roll') != 0", None, consume_next_roll)).silent(),`
- Why: Both the rewrites (`On::Filter` at line 74 and this `On::Action`) are field-card hooks, and hook dispatch walks only `world.field_instances(player_id)` (crates/game-rules/src/wasm_rules.rs:5015, entries from host.rs:202-221). A declared counter leaves the hand (wasm_rules.rs:6054-6060) and is never placed on the field, so neither entry can run. Probe after the counter and a full round: `slot=1`, next main move `plan_base=1d20`, and `slot_after=1` -- the promised 1d6 never lands and the latch never clears.
- Fix: Carry the rewrite as an engine-side lingering/turn-scoped effect that the roll window consults, instead of hand-card Filter/Action hooks.

### `AG:回家的路上绕个道` — wrong_behavior — `rules/cards/card-ag/src/detour.rs:247`
- Sheet: 「进行一次 “afterglow”式的移动（视为使用一次初始Afterglow角色的（2）效果）」
- Code: `apply_plan(player_id, pick);`
- Why: On the counter path the body runs inside `diceRollResult`, but `main_move` cloned `m` from `turn.plan` at play.rs:1346 (before the roll) and after the raise only `t.value` and the plan's `resolve` are pulled back (play.rs:1422, 1443). `plan_before` for `fold_plan_delta` is snapshotted at walk start (play.rs:1607), i.e. after the counter wrote, so the delta is empty and the walk uses the pre-counter `m`. Probe with only the line-64 gate deleted: countering a roll of 3 and answering the plan prompt with the reverse pick left the player at pos 3 moving forward; `set_reverse`/`set_start`/`set_parity` were lost. The counter performs no afterglow move at all.
- Existing TODO: TODO: This used to hard-code the reverse plan and diverged from `play`; it now shares `apply_plan`, which is a behaviour change on the counter path.
- Fix: Write the plan inside the walk's `moveBefore` window, or re-seed `m` from `turn.plan` after the diceRollResult raise.

### `AG:回家的路上绕个道` — wrong_behavior — `rules/cards/card-ag/src/detour.rs:255` [recorded]
- Sheet: 「(2)进行一次 “afterglow”式的移动（视为使用一次初始Afterglow角色的（2）效果），并将下一次的移动掷骰变更为1d6」
- Code: `    ctx::state::set(player_id, NEXT_ROLL, 1);`
- Why: The armed latch is consumed and applied only by the card's field hooks, On::Filter(Filter::DiceRollPlan) (74) and On::Action(Action::DiceRollAfter) (79), and hooks dispatch only over world.field_instances (wasm_rules.rs:4989+). A card played as a counteraction has uid -1 and takes the default Dest::Graveyard (wasm_rules.rs:6327-6338), and neither play_with_plan nor counteract_with_plan calls set_dest(ctx::Dest::Field). So the counter-path rewrite is dead: measured with the counter forced through, the card went to the deck via reshuffle, NEXT_ROLL was still 1 after the next turn's move (never consumed), and that move's plan.base stayed 1d20 instead of 1d6. Comparable one-shot hand cards that need a later hook (sports_talent.rs, kaoru_thief.rs) set Dest::Field and guard with card.placed. The bare TODO at 254 does not mention the zone problem.
- Fix: Keep the counter's card live: set_dest(Dest::Field) when the counter arms NEXT_ROLL plus a TurnEnd that sends it to the graveyard (the sports_talent pattern), or move the rewrite onto a scheduled/global hook.

### `AG:（摩卡）0.5倍速` — wrong_behavior — `rules/cards/card-ag/src/moca_half.rs:41`
- Sheet: 「你的回合结束时移除一个奇迹水晶」
- Code: `        On::Action(Action::TurnEnd("card.placed", None, turn_end)),`
- Why: Confirmed against the sheet and the engine, not just the spec note. The cell says 「你的回合结束时」 (the owner's turn end only). The field-card hook walk in wasm_rules.rs:5003-5032 iterates every player's field for every non-Drawn trigger kind, and WindowCtx actor is t.player_id (cond_pre.rs:1323), so a `card.placed`-only pre fires at *every* seat's turn end. Ran rules/tests/tests/zz_moca_scratch.rs: crystals printed 3 -> 2 after seat 1's turn end, then 2 -> 1 after the owner's. zz_moca_zero.rs likewise discarded the card (1 crystal) on seat 1's turn end. Sibling owner-only hooks (endless_journey.rs:20, sports_talent.rs:20) carry `actor == owner`; fire_bird/j11 omit it because their sheet says 「每回合结束时」, which moca_half's does not. No TODO or BACKLOG entry covers it.
- Fix: Change the pre to "actor == owner && card.placed" (matching endless_journey / sports_talent).

### `AG:（鸫）微小的『能做到』的事` — wrong_behavior — `rules/cards/card-ag/src/tsugumi_can.rs:88`
- Sheet: 「使任意结果只有数字区间的效果以理论最大值或最小值结算」
- Code: `ctx::set_extreme(if pick == 0 { 1 } else { -1 });`
- Why: Confirmed by code path and a live run. HostRequest::Roll (crates/game-rules/src/wasm_rules.rs:4012) calls Cx::card_roll -> Cx::roll (crates/game-core/src/engine/cx.rs:1346), and Cx::roll rolls plain faces and never reads turn.extreme (only World::roll_silent/ops.rs:554 and the unused CardWorld::roll/wasm_rules.rs:696 honour it). Ran the worktree's zz_probe_yolo_extreme: the d6 was rolled while the DBG line showed extreme=1, and it still settled at the natural value (dice_sums=[2], YOLO d4 not maxed). Effect (1) is entirely dead.
- Fix: Make Cx::roll (or card_roll) honour turn.extreme, and assert max/min on a countered roll in a test.

### `AG:（鸫）微小的『能做到』的事` — wrong_behavior — `crates/game-core/src/engine/play.rs:2187`
- Sheet: 「（1）【反击】时机合适时打出此卡，使任意结果只有数字区间的效果以理论最大值或最小值结算，除@Tsugu ycm以外无法直接改变骰子点数（ex：可以对YOLO生效但无法对无论是何种颜色的夕阳生效）」
- Code: `        self.roll(player_id as i32, count.max(0), sides.max(1), None)`
- Why: card_roll delegates to Cx::roll (cx.rs:1346), which rolls the RNG directly and never reads TurnCtx::extreme; only World::roll_silent/do_move_roll honour it. Instrumented probes: with CAN declared and max answered on AG:无论是何种颜色的夕阳, the trace is `DBGCXROLL count=1 sides=6 extreme=1` yet the dice sum stays the loaded 2 (max would be 6); the same happens for YOLO. So effect (1) is a no-op for every `ctx::roll`-based card effect -- the card's headline effect. This is unrecorded: the only extreme TODO (play.rs:2195) is about roll_tables/move rolls, not Cx::roll.
- Fix: Make Cx::roll (or card_roll) settle count/sides at the armed extreme, like roll_silent does, or route card_roll through it.

### `Mujica:人偶的箱庭` — wrong_behavior — `rules/cards/card-mujica/src/doll_garden.rs:43`
- Sheet: 「无法移动的玩家只能选择向你支付。」
- Code: `let can_move = rules_status::stay::get(p) <= 0 && rules_status::stun::get(p) <= 0;`
- Why: The clause keys off the whole [无法移动] gate, but this predicate reads only the `stay` field and the `stun` field. The gate is `stay > 0 && unstoppable == 0` OR `stun + stunStart > 0` OR `exile > 0` (rules/status/src/stay.rs STAY_HOLDS_MOVE, stun.rs STUNNED, exile.rs EXILED). Two cases are missed: (a) a player holding a pending `stunStart` layer (stun::get reads only STUN, and hitoshizuku.rs:49-53 shows stunStart must be read separately) and (b) an [除外] player. Both are currently offered the move branch and get force-walked even though they cannot move; the book, the spec (C-MUJICA-人偶的箱庭-02 names [除外]/[晕眩]) and the status hosts all say they must pay. No TODO(规则书) at the site.
- Fix: Compute cannot-move from the whole gate: `stay>0 && unstoppable<=0` OR `stun + state::get(p, STUN_START) > 0` OR `exile > 0` (or the status-crate blocks_move composites); add TODO(规则书) until a generic guest move-gate reader exists.

### `Mujica:无法将视线移开` — wrong_behavior — `rules/cards/card-mujica/src/cant_look_away.rs:65` [recorded]
- Sheet: 「使当前回合内对你打出过[反击]的所有玩家向你选择的方向强制移动1~4以内的任意步数并[触发结算]」
- Code: `    let counter_user = trigger::player_id();     if counter_user < 0 || counter_user == player_id {         return Ok(());`
- Why: The On::Play entry is dead. wasm_rules.rs `play()` builds the play trigger with `player_id: player_id as i32` (the seat playing the card) and `target: player_id as i32`, so on a normal hand play trigger::player_id() == player_id and the guard returns before any ask or move. A normal play therefore moves nobody even when that same player countered you earlier this turn. Ran the scratch probe: normal play after a 宣战布告 counter gives `P1 before=10 after=10 prompts=0` and fails at zz_probe_look_away.rs:54. The spec test `look_away_only_moves_counteractors` passes only because its single case is the empty counterer set (verified: it passes in 4.95s), where no movement is expected either way. The TODO at 54-63 only describes the counteract path (trigger::player_id() = that link's declarer), so it does not cover this.
- Fix: Branch per entry: on the play entry derive the target set from the turn's counteraction history and only skip when the set is empty; keep the guard only for the counter entry.

### `Mujica:祥，移动` — extra_effect — `rules/cards/card-mujica/src/saki_move.rs:64`
- Sheet: 「触发结算时进行的支付价格减半」
- Code: `    ctx::plan::set_pay_factor(500);`
- Why: pay_factor is written on the same never-reset turn.plan and scale_settle_payment multiplies plan.pay_factor into every non-buy/build payment (play.rs:2503). The forced walk's own settle should be halved, but the actor's later main-move settlement is halved too. Probe zz_saki_probe.rs: rent 320 on the tile the actor reaches afterwards is paid as 160 (money 10000 -> 9840) -- the pay factor was still active in the actor's main move, which the book's clause does not cover.
- Fix: Restore the prior pay_factor after the forced walk (same scope fix as steps/reverse).

### `Mujica:祥，移动` — wrong_behavior — `rules/cards/card-mujica/src/saki_move.rs:59`
- Sheet: 「强制一名玩家向你选择的方向移动3格并[触发结算]」
- Code: `    ctx::plan::set_steps(3);     ctx::plan::set_reverse(!forward);`
- Why: Re-verified independently: set_steps/set_reverse write the shared turn.plan; card_move only seeds it and restores an outer plan for nested moves (play.rs:821-826). Playing the card on another player is not nested, so the shaped plan stays. main_move clones turn.plan (play.rs:1346) and walk reads steps.unwrap_or(roll) (play.rs:1612/1624), so the actor's own later main move walks 3 in the card's direction and ignores the dice. Probe zz_saki_leak2 fails exactly so: after the forced backward walk the actor at 20 with a 5 rolled ends at 17 (backward 3), not 25; zz_saki_probe shows the forward case too (dice 1, actor ends 3 tiles away). The book gives the 3-tile directed walk only to the forced player.
- Fix: Save/restore steps+reverse (and the rest of the walk shape) around ctx::card_move when the walker is not the actor, or have the engine drop a non-main card walk's plan shape.

### `Mujica:祥，移动` — wrong_behavior — `rules/cards/card-mujica/src/saki_move.rs:64`
- Sheet: 「触发结算时进行的支付价格减半」
- Code: `    ctx::plan::set_pay_factor(500);`
- Why: A second observable consequence of the unrestored plan: pay_factor stays 0.5 on turn.plan after the card, main_move clones it, and scale_settle_payment multiplies by turn.plan.pay_factor (play.rs:2503, which is not reset by buy/build/gain skips but applies to every later settle). After targeting another player, the user's own main-move settle also pays half. Same root cause but a separate settlement effect the finding's fix must cover.
- Fix: Restore/clear plan.pay_factor together with steps and reverse after ctx::card_move.

### `Mujica:祥，移动` — wrong_behavior — `rules/cards/card-mujica/src/saki_move.rs:73`
- Sheet: 「强制一名玩家向你选择的方向移动3格并[触发结算]（可在掷骰前选择自己以代替主要移动），触发结算时进行的支付价格减半」
- Code: `    ctx::card_move(hit);`
- Why: The shaped turn plan is never restored after the forced walk. turn.plan keeps steps=Some(3) (line 59), reverse (line 60) and pay_factor=0.5 (line 64); turn.plan is never reset between moves in a turn (play.rs:52-58) and main_move clones it (`let mut m = self.w.turn.plan.clone()`, play.rs:1346) while walk uses `m.steps.unwrap_or(m.roll)` (play.rs:1612/1624). So when another player is targeted the user's pending main move walks 3 tiles in the card's direction ignoring the dice, records main_steps=3, and halves its settle payments. Reproduced: rules/tests/tests/zz_saki_leak2.rs fails, turn player at 20 rolls 5 and ends at 17 instead of 25. No TODO at the site.
- Fix: Save/restore the turn plan around ctx::card_move(hit) (or clear steps/reverse/pay_factor afterwards); there is no set_steps(None)/plan-reset API today, so add one (or a TODO(规则书) at this site).

### `Mujica:（初华）我，无畏悲伤` — wrong_behavior — `rules/cards/card-mujica/src/uika_fearless.rs:30`
- Sheet: 「打出此卡时若自从上一次[经过]CiRCLE后有在任何[回忆地块][触发结算]」
- Code: `    let memory = ctx::state::get(player_id, "memory.dirty");`
- Why: The gate reads a latch that is write-once. Its only writer is rules/skills/skill-personas/src/uika.rs:126 (`state::set(player_id, MEMO_DIRTY, 1)`); that skill's CiRCLE-pass handler (uika.rs:113-116) clears only `skill.uikaImprisoned.dirty`, never `memory.dirty`, and the state key has no turn-start/end expiry. After the first memory-tile settle the latch stays 1 for the rest of the match, so the card offers its choice even when nothing settled since the last CiRCLE -- the gate no longer matches the sheet.
- Fix: Have the skill's on_pass also `state::set(player_id, MEMO_DIRTY, 0)`, or make the card read `skill.uikaImprisoned.dirty` (per-player, already cleared on pass).

### `Mujica:（初华）我，无畏悲伤` — wrong_behavior — `rules/cards/card-mujica/src/uika_fearless.rs:30`
- Sheet: 「打出此卡时若自从上一次[经过]CiRCLE后有在任何[回忆地块][触发结算]，你可选择抽1张卡或使你本回合的移动掷骰结果可定义为1-6以内的任何数字」
- Code: `let memory = ctx::state::get(player_id, "memory.dirty");`
- Why: Verified independently: the sheet scopes the gate to 「自从上一次[经过]CiRCLE后」. `memory.dirty` is written only at rules/skills/skill-personas/src/uika.rs:126 (on every memory-tile settle) and is never cleared: that skill's CiRCLE-pass handler at line 114 does `state::set(player_id, DIRTY, 0)` where DIRTY = "skill.uikaImprisoned.dirty", not MEMO_DIRTY = "memory.dirty". A whole-repo grep for memory.dirty/MEMO_DIRTY finds only the const declaration, that one write, and this read - no reset, no per-turn clear. After the first memory-tile settle the gate therefore stays open for the rest of the game, so every later copy resolves as if 'settled since the last CiRCLE pass' holds. The spec restates the scope at docs/rulebook/spec/cards/mujica.md:345 ("since the last [经过]CiRCLE the user settled at least one [回忆地块]"). No TODO at the site.
- Fix: Clear memory.dirty next to DIRTY in the skill's on_pass (or have the card clear its own key after use if a readable-but-one-shot latch is chosen), then test with two CiRCLE passes.

### `Mujica:（喵梦）` — missing_clause — `rules/cards/card-mujica/src/nyamu_card.rs:55` [recorded]
- Sheet: 「每当此卡将从正面翻至背面时可选择失去2个火罐。」
- Code: `    let now = ctx::self_face_down();     let was = ctx::slot(player_id, WAS_DOWN) != 0;     ctx::set_slot(player_id, WAS_DOWN, now as i32);     if was && !now {`
- Why: Correct: watch_flips only has the `was && !now` (face-up) branch; there is no down-flip branch, no optional ask, and no `bandori_std::fire::spend` anywhere in the file. Clause (2)1 is entirely unimplemented. The existing TODO at line 70 quotes the clause but its `--` half describes what C# does and an interpretation, never that nothing is spent/asked, so the gap is not recorded. The suggested fix needs one more caveat: adding a `!was && now` branch inside watch_flips would still not run, because a face-down placed card's Action hook is skipped (crates/game-rules/src/wasm_rules.rs:5024-5028) and a TurnEnd handler is post-flip anyway — the clause needs an at-flip (pre-flip) hook.
- Existing TODO: // TODO(规则书)[judgement]（2）1: 「每当此卡将从正面翻至背面时可选择失去2个火罐」     //   -- the clause does not say what losing the 2 fire pots buys. C#     //   `CardNyamuCard.Flipped(down: true)` runs `H.AskYes` + `H.SpendFire(Seat, 2)`     //   and then stops there, so the C# is no more specific. Read here as "pay 2     //   fire pots to keep the card face-up"; without that reading the spend is     //   pure loss and th…
- Fix: Add an at-flip/pre-flip hook that asks yes/no and, on yes, spends 2 fire pots (bandori_std::fire::spend), with a TODO(规则书) recording the unsolved "what does the spend buy" reading.

### `Mujica:（喵梦）` — wrong_behavior — `rules/cards/card-mujica/src/nyamu_card.rs:53`
- Sheet: 「每当此卡将从背面翻至正面时此卡拥有者抽一张卡。」
- Code: `    let was = ctx::slot(player_id, WAS_DOWN) != 0;     ctx::set_slot(player_id, WAS_DOWN, now as i32);     if was && !now {`
- Why: Confirmed, but worse than the finding says: not only is the flip sampled as net state at the owner's TurnEnd (losing transitions and deferring), a face-down placed card's On::Action hook is not dispatched at all — crates/game-rules/src/wasm_rules.rs:5024-5028 skips the instance when `card_face_down` is set. WAS_DOWN is written only inside watch_flips, so at every turn end the card is face-up, `now` is always false and `was` is always 0: the (2)2 draw never fires naturally, not merely late. Empirically confirmed with rules/tests/tests/zz_nyamu_flip_probe.rs: `nyamu_natural_flip_up_does_not_draw` ends a face-down round with `wasDown=0` and the hand unchanged (0 -> 0), while `nyamu_up_flip_draws_when_wasdown_seeded` proves the branch itself draws when `wasDown=1` is seeded (0 -> 1). Unrecorded: the prose at lines 76-77 names the C# `Fx.Flipped` but is not a TODO(ABI) and nothing else records it.
- Fix: Raise the trigger at the actual flip (a flip event / pre-flip hook) instead of polling net face state at TurnEnd; if an event is missing, record `TODO(ABI)` naming it in our vocabulary.

### `Mujica:（祥子）斩断留恋，忘却一切` — wrong_behavior — `rules/cards/card-mujica/src/sakiko_cut.rs:37`
- Sheet: 「抵押一张你拥有且未抵押的最贵地契」
- Code: `    if mortgageable(player_id).is_empty() {         return Some(Msg::new(key!("x_no_mortgageable")));     }`
- Why: The mortgage is a mandatory cost, so it is gated only in `cant_play`. That gate is consulted by the engine's hand-press/offer path (play.rs:956, cx.rs:604 say the gate is not re-checked by the run); the deck auto-play does not consult it. rules/skills/skill-personas/src/sakiko.rs:200 autoplays the deck top with `ctx::play_card`, and ctx::play_card (crates/game-rules/src/hostfns.rs:2704-2764) calls the entry with OP_RUN directly, no gate. So when the card comes off the draw pile with no unmortgaged deed, `best` is None, no mortgage is taken, and the card still discards and teleports for free.
- Fix: Re-check `mortgageable` inside the body and bail (or have the auto-play query the press entry) so the mortgage cannot be skipped.

### `Mujica:（祥子）斩断留恋，忘却一切` — wrong_behavior — `rules/cards/card-mujica/src/sakiko_cut.rs:76`
- Sheet: 「抵押一张你拥有且未抵押的最贵地契并弃置一张手牌（若无手牌则弃掉下一张抽到的牌），立刻传送至任意可购买或已拥有的格子并触发结算」
- Code: `    if let Some(t) = best {         // 规则书: 「抵押」 -- C# `H.MortgageRoutine(i, t, ...)`. The engine refuses         // (and logs its own reason) when the deed cannot be mortgaged.         ctx::card_mortgage(player_id, t);`
- Why: The drive's answer log is shared by prompt answers AND host-request return values (`answers.push(v)` in wasm_rules.rs's `NeedHost` arm), and a re-run resumes at cursor 0. With exactly one unmortgaged deed the mortgage commits on pass 1; every replay pass then finds `mortgageable` empty, skips `card_mortgage`, and so leaves its logged `1` at the cursor for the next read. The discard `ask_card` consumes it as its pick and never renders; with an empty hand the `ask_tile` consumes it and the destination is auto-picked. With 2+ deeds the phantom `card_mortgage` on replay consumes the value and keeps alignment, which is why the shipped two-deed tests pass. Unrecorded; the replay note at 56-68 asserts the skipped call is harmless.
- Fix: Issue exactly one `card_mortgage` request per pass (latch the chosen tile across the pause and re-call it even when the list is empty) so the answer cursor cannot slide; add tests with one deed (with and without hand).

### `CRYCHIC:一起演奏音乐的命运共同体` — wrong_behavior — `rules/cards/card-crychic/src/fate_together.rs:95`
- Sheet: 「（1）[手] 直到你的下回合开始，每当场上任意格子发生一次收款时，你移动到你前方的下一个属于行动序列后一名玩家的格子（不触发结算）。」
- Code: `ctx::card_move(player_id); ctx::set_slot(player_id, SLOT_HIT, 1);`
- Why: ctx::card_move runs the move as the player's main move (play.rs:773-800): when the owner is the turn player and main_moved is already true - the ordinary case in which the owner lands on an opponent's tile and pays rent during their own main move - card_move logs log.main_move_used and returns without moving. The hook ignores the return and still writes the hit (and logs moved), so no hop happens and the card goes to the graveyard at the next turn start. Probe: play, land on an opponent tile, pay rent -> pos unchanged, keys [log.main_move_used, cards:card-crychic.fate_together_moved], card not in hand afterwards. The converse also diverges: a hop fired before the owner's main move counts as that main move and consumes it.
- Fix: Run the hop as a non-main move (plan.forced / an engine effect-move path) and/or honour card_move's bool, keeping the card armed when the move is refused.

### `CRYCHIC:一起演奏音乐的命运共同体` — wrong_behavior — `rules/cards/card-crychic/src/fate_together.rs:96`
- Sheet: 「（2）若此卡打出后（1）效果未产生作用，将此卡返回手牌（不触发乐队技能的（3）效果）。」
- Code: `                    ctx::set_slot(player_id, SLOT_HIT, 1);`
- Why: On a collection paid by another player the move is a host request, and the body is re-run from the already-moved position. If the next-in-order player owns no further tile strictly ahead of the landing tile (the ordinary case: they own only the tile just hopped onto), the re-run falls out of the loop before line 96, so the hit write from the first pass is dropped. `fate_hit` remains 0 and at the owner's next turn start the card wrongly returns to hand even though (1) moved the player. Probe zz_fate_hit_probe::probe_exact_one_copy (p1 owns only {5}): pos=5, fate_hit=0, card in hand at next start; control with p1 owning {5,8}: fate_hit=1, card graveyarded.
- Fix: Store the hit on the card instance (`ctx::set_prop`) rather than the shared player slot and move the hit/log before `card_move`; pin the one-tile-ahead case in a test.

### `CRYCHIC:一起演奏音乐的命运共同体` — wrong_behavior — `rules/cards/card-crychic/src/fate_together.rs:115`
- Sheet: 「若此卡打出后 （1）效果未产生作用，将此卡返回手牌（不触发乐队技能的 （3）效果）。」
- Code: `            if ctx::slot(player_id, SLOT_HIT) == 0 {`
- Why: `SLOT_HIT` goes through `ctx::slot` -> `Ops::state_get(player_id, key)`, a free-form **per-player** counter (crates/game-core/src/engine/ops.rs:473-480), and `fate_together` resets it to 0 on every Play. The bit therefore answers "did any copy move since the last Play", not "did THIS instance move". Two copies share one bit and read the same value at the same instant, so they always take the same branch: if copy B is played after copy A hopped, the Play reset makes both copies return to hand at turn start -- the copy that did produce a result is wrongly returned instead of being discarded; if the hop lands after the second Play, both are discarded and the copy that never produced a result is wrongly discarded. (The reviewer's 'the copy that never hopped is discarded' half is backwards: with the shared 0 both copies return.)
- Fix: Key the hit flag to the field instance (uid prop/counter) instead of the player slot, and write it before the move (see the separate replay finding).

### `CRYCHIC:初演大成功` — wrong_behavior — `rules/cards/card-crychic/src/debut_success.rs:31`
- Sheet: 「打出此卡后，本回合内你的资金不会下降（除拍卖与写明不受资金变动效果影响的情况外）」
- Code: `    ctx::set_no_money_loss(player_id);`
- Why: Re-verified. The sheet only forbids the balance falling; it does not abort a purchase. `Cx::money_inner` short-circuits every payment from a locked payer with `Paid::default()` (default `paid: false`) at crates/game-core/src/engine/play.rs:3301-3309, and every spend caller treats that as "the payment failed": `buy` bails at play.rs:2917, the card acquire path at play.rs:1191, and `build` at play.rs:3016 (after `buildBefore` already ran and the `build_discount` layers were already popped). The offers themselves are not suppressed: `can_pay` (engine/cx.rs:1392) ignores the lock, so `offer_buy` (play.rs:2676-2688) and `offer_build` (play.rs:2720-2734) still prompt, and the `deed_buy`/`deed_build` results are discarded. Net effect: for the whole turn the player answers yes, pays nothing, and gets neither deed nor house; a free build discount layer is even consumed. No TODO in the file and no BACKLOG/TEST-FINDINGS entry covers it (grep '初演' finds only CROSS-TESTS.md S4).
- Fix: Make the locked payment complete the spend (settle with loss 0 / `paid = true`) for build/buy/acquire, or add `TODO(规则书)` at line 31.

### `CRYCHIC:去唱卡拉ok吧` — wrong_behavior — `rules/cards/card-crychic/src/karaoke.rs:49`
- Sheet: 「进行至多5次掷骰，并选择其中一个结果作为你本回合的移动掷骰数，视为正常掷骰移动。」
- Code: `results.push(ctx::roll(player_id, 1, 20, RollPurpose::Effect, roll_source::CARD)?.max(0));`
- Why: The sheet calls these faces this turn's movement-roll faces (「你本回合的移动掷骰数」 + 「视为正常掷骰移动」), but both roll sites (49 and 66) hardcode an Effect 1d20 and never consult the move plan. The ABI has RollPurpose::Move precisely for the movement roll: purpose==1 routes to card_do_move_roll, which sums plan.base+plan.dice (wasm_rules.rs:4023-4024 -> play.rs:2200-2205), whereas purpose==0 uses count d sides. Because the chosen face is then pinned with set_fixed_roll, which overrides the plan dice at move time (play.rs:1408), a modified-dice turn is silently ignored: detour's set_base_dice(1,6) still moves 1..20, change_world's 3d20 and STAR BEAT's nd10 are likewise bypassed. No TODO(规则书) and no BACKLOG/TEST-FINDINGS entry records it.
- Fix: Roll each candidate face with RollPurpose::Move (count/sides ignored) so the faces are the turn's movement dice; keep roll_source::CARD and keep the faces in turn_rolls (see note).

### `CRYCHIC:想要成为人类` — wrong_behavior — `rules/cards/card-crychic/src/want_human.rs:111`
- Sheet: 「（2）[自动]若你的回合开始时此卡上拥有两个或以上的奇迹水晶，移除此卡上全部奇迹水晶并使你下次的移动掷骰结果额外增加20-X；若该次移动过程中受到异常移动效果影响，为此卡添加两个奇迹水晶。」
- Code: `                trigger::set_move_roll(roll + boost);`
- Why: The boost is applied only in the DiceRollAfter stage, which runs before the DiceRollResult [反击] reroll window (engine/play.rs:1417-1421). A reroll replaces the value with the fresh face and the boost is lost, although the crystals are already gone and the armed bonus was 「下次的移动掷骰结果」. AG:（绯玛丽）如果并非没问题, the other pure '+N to the result' card, deliberately hooks both ChainKind::DiceRollBefore and DiceRollResult so its +1 survives a reroll (card-ag/src/himari_plus_one.rs:21-27). Reproduced: 2 crystals + X=15 armed +5, roll face 5 -> pos 40; same setup with 立希's reroll played -> pos 32 and the +5 never lands.
- Fix: Keep the boost until after the reroll window (also hook DiceRollResult and add the boost to the new face), instead of consuming it at DiceRollAfter.

### `CRYCHIC:想要抓住...` — missing_clause — `rules/cards/card-crychic/src/want_to_grab.rs:59`
- Sheet: 「你的下回合开始前，当第一位其他玩家经过你，在那名玩家[触发结算]前，你立刻向前移动一格并[触发结算]。」
- Code: `ctx::state::set(player_id, PASSER, -1);     Ok(())`
- Why: The play body never calls `ctx::set_dest(ctx::Dest::Field)`, so `dest_from(DEST_UNSET)` (wasm_rules.rs:9710-9716) maps the play to `Dest::Graveyard` (play.rs:4778-4784 discards it and creates no field instance). Every `On::Action` hook is dispatched only to placed field instances (wasm_rules.rs:5005-5038), so with the card in the discard neither the PassPlayer nor the SettleBefore listener can ever run: clauses (2) and (3) are entirely dead, not merely unruled. Empirically confirmed by the untracked probes in this worktree: `cargo test -p ruleset-tests --test zz_probe_want` fails both — `FIELD=[] HAND=[]` and the owner never steps (`POS0=5`). Sibling cards that rely on hooks all set Field in the play body (want_human.rs:43, fate_together.rs:47, forever.rs:77, miracle.rs:49), and no TODO records the omission. This also makes findings 1 and 2 latent until fixed.
- Fix: Add `ctx::set_dest(ctx::Dest::Field);` before `Ok(())` in `want_to_grab` so the card stays in play for its two hooks.

### `CRYCHIC:想要抓住...` — wrong_behavior — `rules/cards/card-crychic/src/want_to_grab.rs:30`
- Sheet: 「你的下回合开始前」
- Code: `On::Play("", None, want_to_grab),`
- Why: Confirmed. The CardDef registers On::Play, PassPlayer and SettleBefore only; no TurnStart handler and no other disarm exists (grep of the file). PASSER is set at play (line 59) and only ever written again by the grab (line 82), so nothing closes the clause (2) window when the owner's next turn starts; the latch survives indefinitely and can fire several turns later. Clause (3) itself is anchored on 「你在回合开始时」, so a TurnStart handler is also the natural place to close the window.
- Fix: Register an `On::Action(Action::TurnStart("actor == owner && card.placed", ...))` handler that closes PASSER (and drives clause (3)).

### `CRYCHIC:想要抓住...` — wrong_behavior — `rules/cards/card-crychic/src/want_to_grab.rs:35` [recorded]
- Sheet: 「当第一位其他玩家经过你」
- Code: `On::Action(Action::PassPlayer("actor != owner && actor >= 0", None, on_pass)).silent(),`
- Why: Confirmed. play.rs:1931-1945 (overlap_at) raises passPlayer once per (mover, other player on the landing tile) pair, and wasm_rules.rs:5005-5038 drives every player's placed field card for the kind (the loop is over all players, not just the mover). The pre only drops the owner-as-actor, so if A holds this card and B lands on C's tile, A's latch still sets and A's SettleBefore grab moves A. The repo's own owner-gating pattern for this exact clause is `PassTile("actor != owner && tile.id == owner.pos")` (kaoru.rs:55, sayo.rs:41).
- Fix: Gate on the owner: `Action::PassTile("actor != owner && tile.id == owner.pos", None, on_pass)` (or add `&& target == owner` if staying on PassPlayer).

### `CRYCHIC:想要抓住...` — wrong_behavior — `rules/cards/card-crychic/src/want_to_grab.rs:52` [recorded]
- Sheet: 「（2）你的下回合开始前，当第一位其他玩家经过你，在那名玩家[触发结算]前，你立刻向前移动一格并[触发结算]。」
- Code: `    rules_status::stay::add(player_id, 1);`
- Why: The On::Play body only adds [停留] and writes the PASSER slot; nothing calls ctx::set_dest(ctx::Dest::Field) or ctx::place_card, and WasmRules::play returns Dest::Graveyard when the body sets no dest (play.rs:4773, wasm_rules.rs:9326-9332), so the card is pushed to the graveyard. Hook dispatch walks only world.field_instances (wasm_rules.rs:5015), so the declared clause-(2) PassPlayer/SettleBefore entries never run. Probe output: FIELD=[] HAND=[] and, after another player passes, pos(0) stays 5 (zz_probe_want, both tests fail). Minor correction to the claim: the SettleBefore entry belongs to clause (2)'s grab; clause (3) has no TurnStart entry at all. The module-doc line about a held `GrabFx` attachment is not a TODO and the R5/(3) TODOs do not mention placement, so the gap is unrecorded.
- Fix: Call ctx::set_dest(ctx::Dest::Field) and ctx::place_card in want_to_grab, and return/remove the card when the window ends.

### `CRYCHIC:春日影` — wrong_behavior — `rules/cards/card-crychic/src/haruhikage.rs:113`
- Sheet: 「（2）（此卡可作为[反击]打出）使用一次Crychic角色的技能」
- Code: `    if let Some(&id) = CRYCHIC.get(k) {         ctx::play_card(id, player_id)?;`
- Why: `ctx::play_card` runs only the target's `On::Play` (hostfns::play_card looks up `OnKind::Play` and returns untouched when there is none). Of the five skills in the option list, only 高松灯 and 若叶睦 have an `On::Play`: taki_crychic has only TurnStartBefore/DiceRollAfter/CardUsed, soyo_crychic only PaySplitChooseBefore/DiceRollPlan/MoveAfter, sakiko_crychic only fire-declare/Pass/SettleBefore. Picking 椎名立希, 长崎素世 or 丰川祥子 from hand therefore does nothing at all, while the played 春日影 still leaves the hand. Probe: choosing taki logged only `haruhikage_play`, fire stayed 1 and no latch changed; choosing tomori ran `tomori_crychic_spend`/`tomori_crychic_cleared`.
- Fix: Run the skill's actual trigger body for these three (the counter path already does), or route the choice through the same per-skill invocations instead of `play_card`.

### `CRYCHIC:（soyo）回到曾经` — wrong_behavior — `rules/cards/card-crychic/src/soyo_back.rs:111`
- Sheet: 「为你的一个格子付费加盖一间房屋」
- Code: `            let cost = ctx::build_cost(t);             if ctx::pay(                 player_id,                 cost,`
- Why: Confirmed. ctx::build_cost is World::build_cost -> purchase::build_cost (crates/game-core/src/engine/play/purchase.rs:131), i.e. the raw tile.house with no build_cost_pct and no build_discount. ctx::pay sends HostRequest::Pay, which resolves as PayKind::Card with must=true (crates/game-rules/src/wasm_rules.rs:4370-4389); only the build path (play.rs:2979-3002) applies pct/discount, pops a discount layer, and uses PayKind::Build, and only it fires buildAfter + the full-commit refund. Here a 「下次盖房时减免N」 player pays full price and wrongly keeps the layer, the payment is not a build payment, and no buildAfter is raised. Because must=true also skips the PayKind::Build `exclude`, the chosen tile itself can be mortgaged by raise_funds and then gets a house while still mortgaged (play.rs:3736-3743, 4007-4031). G-SETTLE-07 (docs/rulebook/spec/FINDINGS.md:56) makes the build price the deed's marked house-building cost.
- Fix: Pay through ctx::build_cost-aware build path (ctx::card_offer_build), or use ctx::pay_build plus the pct/discount/layer handling and a PayKind::Build payment; note pay_build returns 0 on a free build so the `> 0` gate must be adjusted.

### `CRYCHIC:（灯）内心的呐喊` — wrong_behavior — `rules/cards/card-crychic/src/tomori_inner_shout.rs:104`
- Sheet: 「若你的乐队技能为“MyGO!!!!!”，打出此卡时将其放置于你最早拥有的格子上」
- Code: `if ctx::in_group(player_id, "MyGO!!!!!") {`
- Why: `in_group` is persona-group based (`wasm_rules.rs:2191-2201` maps players.persona -> PersonaData.group); 高松灯（CRYCHIC）'s persona group is CRYCHIC (`rules/personas/src/tomori_crychic.rs:7`) and the CRYCHIC band (2) transform only attaches `skill:MyGO!!!!!:迷途之星` (`skill-groups/src/crychic.rs:213-217`) — no `set_persona` anywhere in the transform. `crychic_variants_cannot_use_base_exclusives` proves the only legal player of this card is the CRYCHIC persona, and that variant can never play base 高松灯's exclusives either, so the persona group stays CRYCHIC forever. The (3) branch is therefore unreachable and the whole placement/pull payoff never happens in real play. Sibling `mutsumi_never.rs:47-49` reads the band with `ctx::group_skills` for exactly this clause.
- Fix: Gate on the held band skill (`ctx::group_skill` / `ctx::group_skills` matching `skill:MyGO!!!!!:*`), not the persona group.

### `HHW:Happy, Lucky, Smile, Yeah！` — wrong_behavior — `rules/cards/card-hhw/src/happy_lucky.rs:72`
- Sheet: 「投掷1d4mod4，对应投掷结果1-4沿上，下，左，右其中之一的方向传送至直线距离最远的格子（例：在弦卷豪宅骰到4则传送到商店街），视为你的主要移动。」
- Code: `    let (x, y) = grid(ctx::player_pos(player_id));     let to = match num {         1 => from_grid(x, 15), // 上         2 => from_grid(x, 0),  // 下         3 => from_grid(0, y),  // 左         _ => from_grid(15, y), // 右 (face 4 -> num 0)`
- Why: The drawn board is the TTS 12x10 path fold (webui/src/scenes/board/Ring.tsx:20-38 `FOLD`, cell 0 = [11,9]), not a 16x16 ring; the client renders tile i at `FOLD[i % 60]`. The helper pair treats every tile as lying on a 16x16 border, so `from_grid`'s `x==0`/`y==0`/`y==15`/else branches answer as if the source were on that abstract column/row. Verified against rules/kani/src/board.rs `TILE_NAMES` and `FOLD`: index 51 is 北泽精肉店 at [8,3]; face 4 yields grid(51)=(15,9), from_grid(15,9)=51, i.e. the source tile, though [11,3] 山吹面包房 (index 48) is farther right; face 1 yields 45 流星堂 at [11,0] while the farthest up is [8,0] Space (index 42). Likewise index 19 艺术学院高中 at [1,6], face 3 returns 19 itself while [0,6] 白雪学园 (index 18) is farther left. The bug is systematic: all right-column indices 46-59 teleport onto themselves on 右, all top-row 30-45 on 上, all left-column 16-30 on 左, all bottom-row 0-15 on 下. Only the four extreme corners and the sheet example's row-1 pair agree between the ring and the fold, and the tests only exercise indices 0 and 29, so they pass.
- Fix: Carry the real cell per tile (port the Ring.tsx FOLD / TTS pathXY into the rules layer or expose it via host) and scan the ray to the board edge in the chosen direction instead of using the 16x16 ring.

### `HHW:笑容大游行` — wrong_behavior — `rules/cards/card-hhw/src/smile_parade.rs:227`
- Sheet: 「当此卡位于格子上时，那格视为与“弦卷集团”格子交换位置，任何玩家在此卡放置的格子上[触发结算]后此卡放入弃牌堆」
- Code: `    ctx::card_settle_at(player_id, dest, true);`
- Why: In an On::Action hook the handler argument is the field-instance holder (wasm_rules.rs collect_hook_jobs / counteract_orig push Call::Hook{player_id: <field seat>}) while the triggering actor is trigger::player_id() (t.player_id). So the (3) redirect settle runs as the card's owner even when 「任何玩家」 else landed. Verified on a 2-seat table with the card bound to tile 10 and P1 landing there: the nested 弦卷集团 agent offer asked P0 (ASKED=[0]) and log.agent_skip carried seat 0, while P1 was the lander. With the agent tile owned this charges/offers the wrong player entirely. The (1) branch is unaffected because its counteract pre is `actor == owner`, so handler arg == settler there.
- Fix: Settle the (3) redirect as `trigger::player_id()` (the settler); keep `player_id` only for the owner-side log.

### `HHW:运动的天赋` — wrong_behavior — `rules/cards/card-hhw/src/sports_talent.rs:69`
- Sheet: 「「此卡位于场上时，每次掷骰获得一次资金」」
- Code: `/// Residual guard for [`on_roll_after`]: C# `m.FixedRoll >= 0` skips the /// reward on a plan's fixed roll (`trigger::move_roll` is the live face). fn roll_after_applies(_player_id: i32) -> bool {     trigger::move_roll().is_some() }`
- Why: bridge_trigger fills `roll: is_roll_kind.then_some(t.value)` for every roll kind (wasm_rules.rs:7193-7210), and diceRollAfter is raised with value = the face, so move_roll() is Some on every diceRollAfter — including a plan's fixed face (which is what t.value holds after `plan.roll = fixed_roll`). The guard therefore never refuses, and a fixed-face main move (ctx::set_fixed_roll from card-crychic/src/karaoke.rs:95, card-mujica/src/uika_fearless.rs:67, skill-personas/rinko.rs:88) both pays the 700 reward and gets its chosen face replaced by reroll_to_ten whenever the fixed face is below 10, because do_move_roll re-sums the plan tables and ignores fixed_roll (engine/ops.rs:498). The comment describes a skip the code does not have.
- Fix: Gate on the plan's fixed face (`plan.fixed_roll == null`, as roll_plan already does) or drop the dead guard and record the extra payout/reroll with TODO(规则书).

### `HHW:运动的天赋` — wrong_behavior — `rules/cards/card-hhw/src/sports_talent.rs:79`
- Sheet: 「此卡位于场上时，每次掷骰获得一次资金，起始为700，每次减少100，奖励下限为100。」
- Code: `    reward(player_id)?;`
- Why: The sheet pays once per dice roll and steps the ladder down once per roll (「每次…每次减少100」). on_roll_after pays once and then calls reroll_to_ten (line 81), whose loop calls ctx::do_move_roll. That path is OP_DO_MOVE_ROLL -> card_do_move_roll (play.rs:2200-2206), which only sums the plan tables and logs a dice line -- it never raises diceRollAfter. roll_direct's `window: false` only removes the separate Roll counteraction link (hostfns.rs:84, wasm_rules.rs:4029-4032); the Action event is a different hook. So a move that rerolls (roll < 10, the common case) pays only 700 and never 700/600/500… docs/rulebook/CROSS-TESTS.md L7 (~1045-1058) states the expected behaviour explicitly: 「pays 运动的天赋's 700, then 600 on its re-roll(s)」. No TODO records the gap.
- Fix: Call reward() once per reroll inside reroll_to_ten's loop (or raise diceRollAfter for card-driven rerolls) and add TODO(规则书) quoting the per-roll ladder.

### `HHW:（kkr）前往笑容集结的地方！` — wrong_behavior — `rules/cards/card-hhw/src/kokoro_circle.rs:54`
- Sheet: 「支付10000资金（视为买地花费）」
- Code: `    let paid = ctx::pay(player_id, 10000, &Msg::new(key!("kokoro_circle_why")))?;`
- Why: Mechanism confirmed end to end: ctx::pay -> HostRequest::Pay -> `Pay::new(asked, PayKind::Card)` (wasm_rules.rs:4366), and `scale_settle_payment` only exempts Buy/Build/ForceBuy/Gain (play.rs:2495, called at 3568); `paid` is the post-scale `Paid.loss` (334-336, 3796-3798), so `paid < 10000` is true whenever any pay_factor<1 is armed and the card is spent without ever being placed (and the payer underpays). The clause 「视为买地花费」 is therefore not in the pipeline at all, and the `kokoro_circle_why` string advertises it. There is no TODO at the site. Caveat found while verifying: all five `plan::set_pay_factor` writers are cross-group with HHW (repaint/RAS, taki/rana/MyGO, sakiko/CRYCHIC, saki_move/Mujica, umiri/Mujica) and legal decks are group-locked (deck.rs:18-31, setup.rs:173), so a single-band HHW deck will not currently trip it -- real but latent, hence medium rather than high. Note the suggested pay_buy fix is not complete on its own: PayBuy leaves `p.must = false`, dropping the raise-funds/bankrupt path the current ctx::pay takes.
- Fix: Resolve CiRCLE before paying and pay through a Buy-kind path (ctx::pay_buy(player_id, circle, 10000, BuyKind::Card)), keeping the mandatory shortfall behaviour, and cite the built-in kind.

### `HHW:（kkr）前往笑容集结的地方！` — wrong_behavior — `rules/cards/card-hhw/src/kokoro_circle.rs:114`
- Sheet: 「若其他玩家在该格[触发结算]则向所有者支付6000资金，视为格子的收款」
- Code: `    ctx::transfer(`
- Why: Verified: `transfer` -> `HostRequest::Pay` -> `Pay::new(asked, PayKind::Card)` (wasm_rules.rs:4366), and `TriggerPay::new(rent)` gets `rent = p.kind == PayKind::Rent` (play.rs:3342) = false; `ctx::pay_is_rent` documents 'Always false on a card-driven payment' (ctx.rs:2994-2998). So 「视为格子的收款」 is unmodeled: the HHW band skill (1) PaySplitChooseBefore filter and (2) `after_pay` guard both key on `pay_is_rent` (skill-groups/src/hhw.rs:34,62), and rent PaySplitAdd riders (`tile.owner == owner`, `tile.id >= 0`) and `paid_in_settle` never see it. The doc comment at 78-82 explicitly claims `kind = "rent"`, which the code does not do. The nearby TODO at 108-112 is only the CircleNormal move-tag gap (recorded as ABI-02), not this one.
- Fix: Run the 6000 through a fixed-amount `PayKind::Rent` path (with the CiRCLE tile on the payload), or add a named TODO(ABI) for the missing rent/collection card-payment kind.

### `HHW:（美咲）` — wrong_behavior — `rules/cards/card-hhw/src/misaki_card.rs:22`
- Sheet: 「使用火罐进行移动掷骰后」
- Code: `        "actor == owner && move.tag('fireRoll') != 0 && between(owner) > 0",`
- Why: No production code ever writes the `fireRoll` move tag; the only writer is the test fixture `TEST:fireRoll` (rules/fixtures/test-cards/src/lib.rs:586-591). The real fire-pot reroll (rules/skills/skill-personas/src/layer.rs:78, `roll_source::CHARGE`) never tags the plan. At the outer diceRollResult window the trigger's `roll_source` is never stamped either (the `raise!` at play.rs:1421 passes no source; `Trigger::new` leaves `Unattributed`, and only the `roll` host request stamps it, wasm_rules.rs:4031-4033), so switching to `roll_source == 1` alone would still read 0 here. Verified two probes: with 和奏瑞依's real fire reroll kept (fire spent), `counteract_offered("HHW:（美咲）")` is false; planting the fixture tag on the same real-reroll scenario flips it to true, so the tag is the sole blocker and `between()` does fire on the rerolled face.
- Fix: Key the gate on a signal every fire-pot roll producer writes (a plan tag set by the charge-roll producer, and/or thread `roll_source` onto the diceRollResult trigger), not on a tag only the fixture writes.

### `HHW:（美咲）` — wrong_behavior — `rules/cards/card-hhw/src/misaki_card.rs:22`
- Sheet: 「使用火罐进行移动掷骰后，触发结算前可打出此卡」
- Code: `"actor == owner && move.tag('fireRoll') != 0 && between(owner) > 0"`
- Why: Confirmed. Grep over rules/ and crates/ finds no production `set_tag("fireRoll", ...)`; the only writer is the TEST:fireRoll fixture (rules/fixtures/test-cards/src/lib.rs:589). The real fire reroll (rules/skills/skill-personas/src/layer.rs:78) does `ctx::roll(..., RollPurpose::Move, roll_source::CHARGE)` and never tags the plan; misaki/rinko/masking fire spends do not tag either. Re-ran rules/tests/tests/zz_misaki_probe2.rs (real 和奏瑞依 fire reroll, rival at 11 between start 10 and rerolled face): prints `offered=false`; the fixture-based zz_misaki_probe prints `offered=true`. Also verified roll_source is only stamped on the inner card-driven Roll link (game-rules/src/wasm_rules.rs:4033) and never copied onto the main move's diceRollResult trigger, so the tag is the only intended signal and it is dead.
- Fix: Make the fire reroll/skills write `plan::set_tag("fireRoll", 1)` (or carry the nested roll's CHARGE source onto the move result) so the pre can ever match.

### `HHW:（美咲）` — wrong_behavior — `rules/cards/card-hhw/src/misaki_card.rs:82`
- Sheet: 「消耗所有火罐使你传送至你选择的一名位于你的移动起点与预定移动终点之间的玩家所在的格子并触发结算（视为你的主要移动）」
- Code: `    trigger::set_cancelled();     // C# `H.TeleportMove(m2)` with `TeleportTo = to`, `Resolve` default true,     // `Main = m.Main` (「视为你的主要移动」). `card_move` runs the teleport.     ctx::plan::set_kind(MoveKind::Teleport);     ctx::plan::set_teleport_to(to);     ctx::plan::set_resolve(true);     // 规则书[反击]: 「（视为你的主要移动）」 -- C# `m2.Main = m.Main`; `card_move`     // (`MainMoveAs`) runs the teleport n…`
- Why: The teleport never happens. `main_move` sets `turn.main_moved = true` at play.rs:1340 before raising the dice windows, so a `card_move` from the diceRollResult window takes the early-return at play.rs:790-799 (`as_main && self.w.turn.main_moved`), logs `log.main_move_used`, clears `teleport_to` and returns without moving. `trigger::set_cancelled()` only writes the trigger's `negation`, and the roll site (play.rs:1421-1422) never reads it, so the walk then proceeds with the original face. Re-ran the reviewer's probe under rules/tests/tests/zz_misaki_probe.rs: position goes 10 -> 13 with keys ...misaki_card_pay, log.outcome.plan_teleport, log.main_move_used, misaki_card_moved, log.pay, log.roll; the 500 is collected around tile 12 while the mover ends on 13, and the original landing also settles. (`ctx::card_move` returns a Flow/Asked, not a bool, but the returned value is indeed discarded.)
- Fix: Run the teleport as the in-flight main move (treat a mid-roll card_move as the same main move / use the teleport_as path) and make the roll site consume the cancelled trigger so the original walk stops.

### `HHW:（花音）Wacha Mocha 啪嗒进行曲` — wrong_behavior — `rules/cards/card-hhw/src/kanon_march.rs:23`
- Sheet: 「主要阶段中，若此卡上有至少9个水母标记，可以清除所有标记」
- Code: `// One Play entry for both contexts (the engine dispatches only the         // first): [场] placement from hand, or the ≥9-marker jump once placed.         On::Play("", Some(cant_play), play),`
- Why: The jump needs a press of the placed copy, but the client's press surface only lists `skill:*` instances: MatchView.skills filters `!id.starts_with("skill:")` (crates/game-core/src/engine/mod.rs:1316) and the AI reads the same list. Sending the hand act for a placed card fails `err.no_such_card` (engine cant_play, play.rs:4662). Probe: after place_raw, view_extra_typed(0).skills == [] and play(0, WACHA) == Err("err.no_such_card"); the jump is reachable only by playing a second hand copy, which then leaves the field copy discarded. No TODO/BACKLOG item records this.
- Fix: Expose placed cards with an activatable On::Play on the pressable surface (include non-`skill:` field instances in the gated skills list, or add a card-press act), and add TODO until then.

### `HHW:（花音）Wacha Mocha 啪嗒进行曲` — wrong_behavior — `rules/cards/card-hhw/src/kanon_march.rs:23`
- Sheet: 「主要阶段中，若此卡上有至少9个水母标记，可以清除所有标记并传送」
- Code: `        On::Play("", Some(cant_play), play),`
- Why: A placed non-`skill:` card's On::Play entry is not offered anywhere. The viewer's bash view filters field instances to `skill:` ids only (crates/game-core/src/engine/mod.rs:1316), the web UI's only `act: "skill"` sender is the skill stack built from that list (webui/src/scenes/board/Side.tsx:218), and bot AI only considers `skill_rules_of(persona)` (engine/ai.rs:396). Verified: after placing the card, `view_extra_typed(0).skills == []`. So the two pinned tests can press via the test harness but no human client or bot ever can; the card's whole second clause is dead in real play.
- Fix: Expose placed cards whose rule has an `On::Play` entry (they branch on `is_placed()`) in the pressable list, or give the jump a pressable surface.

### `HHW:（花音）Wacha Mocha 啪嗒进行曲` — wrong_behavior — `rules/cards/card-hhw/src/kanon_march.rs:73` [recorded]
- Sheet: 「花音每次倒走时此卡获得一个水母标记」
- Code: `ctx::add_tok(player_id, JELLY, 1, 9)?;`
- Why: The hook is On::Action(Action::Pass(...)) (line 27). Pass fires once per stepped path tile incl. destination (crates/card-sdk/src/abi.rs:1774-1778; raise per tile in engine/play.rs:1754), so one backward walk grants one mark per tile, not one per walk. The card's own cited source is C# `CardKanonMarch.Arrive`, and this repo documents C# Arrive as a once-per-move event (card-sumimi/childhood_cheer.rs:83 'C# `AfterMoveFireFx.Arrive` -- after the owner's main move'). Confirmed by probe (zz_kanon_review2 backward_move_marker_count). docs/rulebook/COVERAGE.md:321 says 'per backward step', but that is a derived summary, not the sheet. No TODO(规则书) at the site.
- Fix: Grant the mark once per completed backward non-teleport move (MoveAfter/arrival shape) instead of per Pass tile; add TODO(规则书) until then.

### `HHW:（花音）Wacha Mocha 啪嗒进行曲` — wrong_behavior — `rules/cards/card-hhw/src/kanon_march.rs:106`
- Sheet: 「传送到#4水族馆或者 #30弦卷豪宅，视为本次主要移动(喊出呼诶诶～!)，然后此卡置入弃牌堆。」
- Code: `    ctx::plan::set_kind(MoveKind::Teleport);     ctx::plan::set_teleport_to(pick);     ctx::plan::set_resolve(true);     ctx::set_dest(ctx::Dest::Graveyard);`
- Why: The body only shapes the turn plan; no ctx::card_move runs now. Probed: after the press the card is in the graveyard, markers 0, the player has not moved, and the jump only happens when a later roll reaches main_move (probe before_roll_press_then_roll: pos unchanged after press, then aiqquarium after roll). Two ordinary states break outright: (a) skill presses are accepted off-turn (why_not_act's skill branch has no turn check), so a P0 press during P1's OPS cleared P0's marks and discarded P0's card while P1's roll teleported P1 (probe_out_of_turn_press / out_of_turn_controlled_dice); (b) with skip_move no roll is owed, t.roll returns Err("status.move_blocked"), the turn ends and the player never teleports (probe_skip_move_turn: pos stayed 29, tok 0, card gone). The book's clear→teleport→discard order is also inverted (set_dest fires at press), and main_move raises diceRollBefore before the teleport branch, so roll windows fire for a move the book gives no roll. No TODO at the site.
- Fix: Run the move now like Happy, Lucky, Smile, Yeah！: keep set_kind/set_teleport_to/set_resolve then ctx::card_move(player_id) before set_dest(Graveyard).

### `HHW:（花音）Wacha Mocha 啪嗒进行曲` — wrong_behavior — `rules/cards/card-hhw/src/kanon_march.rs:106`
- Sheet: 「主要阶段中，若此卡上有至少9个水母标记，可以清除所有标记并传送到#4水族馆或者 #30弦卷豪宅，视为本次主要移动(喊出呼诶诶～!)」
- Code: `ctx::plan::set_kind(MoveKind::Teleport);     ctx::plan::set_teleport_to(pick);     ctx::plan::set_resolve(true);`
- Why: The body only writes the turn plan and never calls ctx::card_move, so the move runs later inside the turn player's main_move (engine/play.rs:1390-1400 consumes plan.teleport_to). The press path (why_not_act 'skill', engine/mod.rs:2966-2986) checks only step==OPS/gates and cant_play does not check turn, so an out-of-turn press is accepted, markers are cleared, the card is discarded, and then the TURN PLAYER is teleported by their own roll. Probe zz_kanon_probe::probe_out_of_turn_press: press on P1's turn accepted, P0 pos 29->29, tok 0, then P1 roll -> P1 pos 3. Sibling main-move cards (be_strongest.rs:26, overlapping_voices.rs:68, balloon_show.rs:26, happy_lucky.rs:28) all gate with rules_status::move_blocked. No TODO.
- Fix: Gate cant_play with rules_status::move_blocked(player_id) (fixes out-of-turn and move-blocked presses); the plan/roll route is otherwise the engine's intended shape for this card.

### `HHW:（花音）Wacha Mocha 啪嗒进行曲` — wrong_behavior — `rules/cards/card-hhw/src/kanon_march.rs:106`
- Sheet: 「可以清除所有标记并传送到#4水族馆或者 #30弦卷豪宅，视为本次主要移动(喊出呼诶诶～!)，然后此卡置入弃牌堆」
- Code: `    ctx::plan::set_kind(MoveKind::Teleport);     ctx::plan::set_teleport_to(pick);     ctx::plan::set_resolve(true);     ctx::set_dest(ctx::Dest::Graveyard);`
- Why: The jump only writes the turn plan; nothing consumes it. `use_skill` (play.rs:959) runs rules.play and never moves, and only `ctx::card_move` emits HostRequest::Move. Verified with a probe: start on tile 10, press at 9 markers, pick aquarium (#3) -> pos stays 10 (markers cleared, card discarded). The stale `teleport_to` is then cloned by the next `main_move` (play.rs:1346/1390), so a later roll teleports instead of walking: with a loaded 20 forced, P0 still ended on 3. `wacha_jump_at_nine_markers` passes vacuously: P0's persona start is #30 弦卷豪宅 (rules/personas/src/kanon.rs:6 text 「游戏开始起点为#30弦卷豪宅」) and the test accepts either tile, so staying put satisfies it.
- Fix: Call `ctx::card_move(player_id)` after shaping the plan (as happy_lucky does) so the jump runs as the main move, and gate the press on the main move being available.

### `Mor:夏日合宿` — wrong_behavior — `rules/cards/card-morfonica/src/summer_camp.rs:77`
- Sheet: 「当此卡效果结束，你没有因为此卡效果无效化任何影响则抽一张牌」
- Code: `    let blocked = ctx::slot(player_id, SLOT_BLOCKED);     ctx::set_slot(player_id, SLOT_BLOCKED, 0);     if blocked == 0 {         ctx::draw(player_id, 1)?;`
- Why: `SLOT_BLOCKED` is read at 75, zeroed at 76, and the draw is gated on `blocked == 0`, but nothing in the repo ever increments/writes it: `grep -rn summer_camp_blocked` finds only lines 17/49/75/76, and the only producer of the shield, `untargetable` (58-61), just does `g.allowed = Some(false)`. So `blocked` is always 0 and the owner draws even when the shield negated a designation. The book lets the draw happen only when nothing was invalidated; CROSS-TESTS C10 says 'P1 negated something, so P1 does not draw' and spec C-MOR-summer-02 conditions it on 'no effect was negated'. The existing test only covers the nothing-negated path, so it does not catch this. `ctx::state::add` (crates/card-sdk/src/ctx.rs:1760) is indeed filter-safe (no `refuse_if_filter`), so the gate can bump the counter.
- Fix: Increment the slot in `untargetable` (e.g. `ctx::state::add(player_id, SLOT_BLOCKED, 1)`) so the draw gate is meaningful.

### `Mor:秘密与青春的虹彩` — wrong_behavior — `rules/cards/card-morfonica/src/secret_rainbow.rs:132`
- Sheet: 「（1）当你向学妹或同级生支付时，打出此卡，此次支付金额减半。」
- Code: `.find(|(name, _, _)| ctx::persona_is(player_id, name))`
- Why: persona_is is exact string equality against MatchPlayer.persona (game-rules/src/wasm_rules.rs:2183). Variant personas store the suffixed name (若叶睦（CRYCHIC）, 三角初华（Sumimi）, etc.; rules/personas/src/*crychic.rs, uika_sumimi.rs), so grade_of returns None for all 6 variants - exactly the only roster names absent from GRADES. can_counteract then short-circuits false and neither clause works when either side of the payment is a variant. No TODO and no BACKLOG entry records it.
- Fix: Add the 6 variant names to GRADES (same ordinal as their base), or normalise the persona before lookup; variant_of is host-side only (game-core/src/data.rs:455), not exposed to cards.

### `Mor:纯真振翅` — wrong_behavior — `rules/cards/card-morfonica/src/pure_wings.rs:50`
- Sheet: 「立刻进行移动掷骰」
- Code: `ctx::plan::set_kind(card_sdk::abi::MoveKind::Walk);     ctx::plan::set_teleport_to(-1);     ctx::plan::set_resolve(true);     ctx::card_move(player_id);`
- Why: The shared turn.plan is never reset between moves, and card_move_seeded only raises diceRollPlan/Value/After/Result when plan.steps.is_none() (play.rs:868). The body clears kind/teleport_to/resolve but never steps, so any earlier card that fixed a step count turns 「移动掷骰」 into a plain walk of the stale length. Reproduced with Mujica:祥，移动 (leaves steps=Some(3), reverse=true): pure_wings teleports 5→45 (backwards) then walks 3→42 and emits no diceRollPlan/Value/After/Result. Baseline on a fresh plan rolls correctly and the spec test passes, so the defect is specifically the stale plan. No TODO at the site; TEST-FINDINGS:338 ("纯真振翅 never rolls after the teleport") is stale as a general claim (the plain test now passes) and is not BACKLOG.
- Fix: Clear plan.steps to "roll it" before card_move (needs an ABI for steps=None); at minimum add TODO(规则书)（立刻进行移动掷骰）/TODO(ABI) at 50-53 naming the stale-plan gap.

### `Mor:蝴蝶飞舞的星月夜` — wrong_behavior — `rules/cards/card-morfonica/src/starry_night.rs:49`
- Sheet: 「支付X次1000的的资金」
- Code: `    let max = (money / 1000).clamp(1, 5);`
- Why: The ask's upper bound is derived from cash on hand only, while `ctx::pay` for a card body runs the must=true raise-funds path (wasm_rules.rs:4390). An owner with 1,900 cash and mortgageable deeds can afford 2 × 1,000 but is offered at most 1, so they can never stock the crystals the book lets them buy. Sibling crimson_soul (rules/cards/card-ag/src/crimson_soul.rs:77-89) already settled this shape: the C# cash cap is a leftover of the removed play gate and the choice must not be pre-capped by cash on hand. No TODO records it.
- Fix: Bound the ask by realisable funds (cash + mortgage value), or ask 1..=5 and let the shortfall path decide.

### `Mor:（Rui）正论恶魔` — extra_effect — `rules/cards/card-morfonica/src/rui_devil.rs:40`
- Sheet: 「在此卡结算过程中，每当你的技能被触发后，立即将x设置为5」
- Code: `ctx::set_slot(player_id, X, 5);`
- Why: The trailing unconditional write applies even when no skill fired during the resolution. Proven: rules/tests/tests/zz_rui_trace.rs `rui_no_skill_still_writes_x` (skills stripped) prints `skill_fired=false x=5`. The spec itself says (spec_card_mor.rs:597-599) the clause is a no-op without a trigger and 'the card itself must not invent the write'. Severity raised from low because the spec explicitly pins the no-op and the probe demonstrates the violation.
- Fix: Guard the trailing write on 'a skill fired this resolution', or move the whole write onto a skill-trigger hook.

### `Mor:（Rui）正论恶魔` — missing_todo — `rules/cards/card-morfonica/src/rui_devil.rs:29`
- Sheet: 「向全场玩家支付100资金（该数值不可被任何效果影响）」
- Code: `ctx::transfer(player_id, o, 100, &why)?;`
- Why: The 100-out legs use ctx::transfer -> sys::pay_to, i.e. the ordinary money pipeline, so 八潮瑠唯's crit skill fires on them and can void or double the loss. Proven empirically: target/debug/deps/zz_rui_fixed_probe (x=19) prints p1=-100 on many seeds instead of the nominal -200 (e.g. seed 0/1), i.e. the 100 to that player was voided -- directly violating 「该数值不可被任何效果影响」. The file has no TODO at all and neither BACKLOG.md nor TEST-FINDINGS.md mentions this card (grep empty). The prose at 26-27 is not a TODO and does not quote the clause. COVERAGE.md even marks #1 'ok'.
- Fix: Add TODO(规则书)（向全场玩家支付100资金（该数值不可被任何效果影响））: the book pins the 100 at face value -- transfer runs the full pay pipeline, so crit/modifiers change it; mark TODO(ABI) fixed-amount transfer if the ABI is the blocker.

### `Mor:（Rui）正论恶魔` — wrong_behavior — `rules/cards/card-morfonica/src/rui_devil.rs:29`
- Sheet: 「「向全场玩家支付100资金（该数值不可被任何效果影响）」」
- Code: `ctx::transfer(player_id, o, 100, &why)?;`
- Why: The 100 leg rides the ordinary pay pipeline (ctx::transfer -> HostRequest::Pay -> cx.money, which raises the `pay` chain link), so the user's own 「正论暴击」 fires on it and modifies the face value, although the sheet pins the 100 and mor.md:563/566 call it immune. Mechanism correction: the trigger value at the `pay` link is positive (play.rs:3577 `value = amount`), so a hit takes rui's `amount * 2` branch and pays 200, it does not void the leg. Probe seed 0: first log.pay amount=200 and p1 ends -100 against the sheet's -200.
- Fix: Route the 100 through a no-modifier pay leg once the host exposes one, or leave an explicit TODO until then.

### `MyGO:即使迷茫着` — wrong_behavior — `rules/cards/card-mygo/src/even_lost.rs:57`
- Sheet: 「（2）[持续] 主要阶段中，你可将此卡置入弃牌堆并进入移动阶段，使你的此次主要移动格数为你当前手牌张数。」
- Code: `    if !ctx::is_placed() {`
- Why: Confirmed by code and by execution. can_go is the only gate on the (2) press and checks placement alone; why_not_act's "skill" branch (crates/game-core/src/engine/mod.rs:2964) gates only on stage::OPS, so it never asks whether the main move is still available. `go` discards first (line 65) and only then calls card_move, which returns Ok without moving once `turn.main_moved` is set (play.rs:790-800). The common after-a-roll case is incidentally blocked because the completed roll leaves step != OPS (probe: err.skill_not_now), but a skip_move turn stays in OPS and the press is accepted: probe skip_move_press returned Ok(()), moved pos 0->3 and set main_moved=true on a turn whose main move is locked. An as-main teleport earlier in OPS leaves main_moved set with step still OPS and is the same failure. Both comparable main-move cards use move_blocked (effort.rs:38, anon_tokyo.rs:151); the comment on play.rs:812 also says a `card_move` must not run as main when the main move is unavailable.
- Fix: Return `rules_status::move_blocked(player_id)` from can_go (in addition to the is_placed check) so the press is refused once the main move is spent, the turn is not yours, or skip_move is latched.

### `MyGO:哪怕这旅程没有终点` — wrong_behavior — `rules/cards/card-mygo/src/endless_journey.rs:103`
- Sheet: 「触发结算时，获得X*60资金，X为你此次主要移动[经过]的格数」
- Code: `    // -- C# `m.Path.Count` is the tiles this main move walked (the trigger     // payload's `move_total`).     let x = trigger::move_total();`
- Why: Confirmed by code and by a black-box run. `trigger::move_total()` reads `trigger().mv.total` (hostfns.rs:2500-2502), and `total` is the *planned* walk: play.rs:1710-1713 recomputes `planned = steps + extra_steps` and assigns `m.total = planned` each step, and the 强制停下 break at play.rs:1769-1771 leaves it at the full length. The entered tiles are `path.len()` = `move_total() - move_remaining()`. Scratch test: journey on P0, Kasumi force-stop tile on P1, main roll 6, stopped after 2 tiles, settle on the stop tile -> payout 360 (planned 6 x 60) although only 2 tiles were 经过 (120). The permanent `journey_settle_gains_x_times_60` only covers an uninterrupted walk, where planned == passed.
- Fix: Use `let x = trigger::move_total() - trigger::move_remaining();` (tiles actually entered) so a forced-stop walk pays only what it passed.

### `MyGO:壱雫空` — wrong_behavior — `rules/cards/card-mygo/src/hitoshizuku.rs:43`
- Sheet: 「清除场上所有[停留]与[眩晕]效果」
- Code: `let locked = ctx::slot(j, "lockedStay").max(0);         let keep = locked.min(stay);`
- Why: `lockedStay` is written only by `MyGO:（灯）不再迷茫` (tomori_no_longer.rs:136, `ctx::inc_slot(player_id, "lockedStay", 2)`) and is read only here. `inc_slot` -> `state_add` declares no `expires`; `tick_state` (game-core/src/state.rs:773-782) only decrements keys whose `expires` is set, and there is no card/engine hook that lowers it. It is therefore a permanent high-water mark: after one 灯 play this card stops clearing that player's ordinary stay for the rest of the match. Concrete: P0 plays 灯 (stay=2, lockedStay=2); two turn-ends decay stay to 0 while lockedStay stays 2; a later [停留] of 1 lands; keep=min(2,1)=1, nothing is cleared or counted, and P0 pays 0 for it, though the clause says all [停留] are cleared. No test covers locked + decay (mygo.rs `clear_removes_all_stay_and_stun` sets bare `stay` only). No TODO/BACKLOG/TEST-FINDINGS entry records it.
- Fix: Decrement `lockedStay` when a locked layer naturally wears off (or keep it in sync with `stay` on decay) and add a 灯-then-decay-then-hitoshizuku test.

### `MyGO:壱雫空` — wrong_behavior — `rules/cards/card-mygo/src/hitoshizuku.rs:96`
- Sheet: 「所有玩家因本效果每清除一种效果则支付此卡使用者1000资金」
- Code: `        if j == player_id {             // Self-pay nets nothing; keep the log so the count is visible.             ctx::log(                 player_id,                 &Msg::new(key!("hitoshizuku_pay")).player_id("who", j),             );         } else {             ctx::transfer(j, player_id, 1000 * n, &Msg::new(key!("hitoshizuku_pay")))?;         }`
- Why: The clause says every player pays the user; the user is one of them. The code special-cases self to a bare log, so the user's own leg never enters `Cx::money`. crates/game-core/src/engine/play.rs:3289-3293 (added in 1e9decf7) explicitly states a self-payment 「ApayA」 is NOT a no-op: the counteraction window, paySplit modifiers and affordability/raise-funds path still apply. With the skip, the user's own cleared types open no `pay`/`paid` window, cannot be re-targeted by a counteraction, and never force raise-funds even when cash is short. The extra `ctx::gain` covers only the net amount, not the payment semantics. No TODO(规则书) and no BACKLOG item; the older Kani spec (rules/kani/src/spec/mygo.rs:281, `j != user`) froze the wrong reading.
- Fix: Drop the self branch: run every `(j, n)` through `ctx::transfer(j, player_id, 1000 * n, ...)`; the self pair nets 0 but keeps the window/modifiers/affordability, as the play.rs Q4 rule requires.

### `MyGO:无路矢` — extra_effect — `rules/cards/card-mygo/src/no_road.rs:112`
- Sheet: 「[除外]期间本应获得的格子收入由此前指定的那名玩家获得」
- Code: `ctx::place_card(player_id, "MyGO:无路矢", &Msg::new(key!("no_road_log")));`
- Why: Confirmed. Dest::Field means the engine never moves the card (play.rs:4794 is a no-op); no body path calls unplace/undesignate, so the field instance and slot('noRoad.who') outlive the exile. redirect_applies only tests exile > 0 (line 136), and the pre still matches card.placed + a valid stale slot, so any LATER exile from another source reroutes that owner's rent to the old pick. Probe review_card_lingers_and_leaks_redirect_into_later_exile passes (P1, the stale pick, gets the 260). Spec C-MYGO-murosshi-04 Condition also says 「from this card」. Nothing in BACKLOG/TEST-FINDINGS records it.
- Fix: Retire the card (unplace / undesignate / clear the slot) when its exile reaches 0, or bind the hook to this card's own exile grant.

### `MyGO:无路矢` — wrong_behavior — `rules/cards/card-mygo/src/no_road.rs:112`
- Sheet: 「获得2层[除外]并在[除外]层数归0后[传送]至该格子」
- Code: `ctx::set_dest(ctx::Dest::Field); ctx::place_card(player_id, "MyGO:无路矢", &Msg::new(key!("no_road_log")));`
- Why: The field stand-in that carries the redirect hook is placed for the exile and nothing ever removes it. The exile tick (rules/status/src/exile.rs:143-171) only clears exileTo/exileMain/exileHold and teleports; it never unplaces the card or undesignates the tile. `destroy_instance_units` runs only when an instance leaves the field (crates/game-core/src/engine/ops.rs:1509), and the card never leaves. Because `redirect_applies` is only `exile > 0` (line 136), the next [除外] from any unrelated source re-arms the redirect to the stale `noRoad.who`, and the [指定] mark stays on the tile for the rest of the game. The spec's own condition for the clause (docs/rulebook/spec/cards/mygo.md C-MYGO-murosshi-04: "Condition: the user holds [除外] layers from this card") is exactly what the `exile > 0`-only guard fails. Verified by running `cargo test -p ruleset-tests --test zz_review_no_road` (4 passed): `review_card_lingers_and_leaks_redirect_into_later_exile` asserts the card is still on_field after its exile ends and then a fresh exile makes the old designatee collect the rent (P1 +260, P0 0).
- Fix: Retire the stand-in when the exile ticks to 0 (a settle hook on the exile-over point, or an exile-count listener that unplaces the card and calls `ctx::undesignate_tile(tile)`), so the mark and the redirect both end with 「[除外]期间」.

### `MyGO:那天的雨` — wrong_behavior — `rules/cards/card-mygo/src/that_day_rain.rs:123`
- Sheet: 「每回合结束时移除一个奇迹水晶，移除所有奇迹水晶后将其放入弃牌堆」
- Code: `            if trigger::player_id() != player_id {                 return Ok(());             }             ctx::remove_crystal()?;`
- Why: Verified independently. The sheet says 「及你的每回合开始时」 (owner-scoped) for the roll and 「每回合结束时」 (unscoped) for the decay; docs/rulebook/spec/cards/mygo.md C-MYGO-rainday-03 rules explicitly 'every player's turn end, not only the owner's', and R:Fire bird applies the same reading (spec/cards/r.md C-R-Fire bird-02, and fire_bird.rs TurnEnd has no actor guard). Engine-wise the hook does fire on every turn end: play.rs raises "turnEnd" for the ending seat i, and wasm_rules.rs walks every player's field dispatching Call::Hook with player_id = field owner, so the body's player_id is the card's holder and trigger::player_id() is the actor; this early return drops all non-owner turn ends. Net: one tick per round instead of per turn end.
- Fix: Delete the `trigger::player_id() != player_id` early return in the TurnEnd arm so every turn end removes one crystal; `is_placed` already gates the hook and the discard guard keys off the counter's owner.

### `MyGO:那天的雨` — wrong_behavior — `rules/cards/card-mygo/src/that_day_rain.rs:123`
- Sheet: 「每回合结束时移除一个奇迹水晶」
- Code: `if trigger::player_id() != player_id {                 return Ok(());             }`
- Why: The sheet cell scopes only the roll with 「你的每回合开始时」 and leaves the decay as 「每回合结束时」, so the crystal must burn at every player's turn end. The spec ruling C-MYGO-rainday-03 says exactly that (mygo.md:572-573), while the structural analog fire_bird.rs installs `On::Action(Action::TurnEnd("card.placed", None, turn_end))` with no owner filter. Here the TurnEnd arm returns early for every non-owner turn, so the card loses one crystal only per OWN turn and survives 3-4x longer in a 3-4 player game while still spraying [停留] every own turn start. Verified that the dispatcher iterates every player's field cards on a TurnEnd (wasm_rules.rs:5009-5046), so an unconditional tick would fire.
- Fix: Drop the trigger-player check in the TurnEnd arm (tick unconditionally while placed, as fire_bird does); keep the owner check only in TurnStart.

### `MyGO:难以复刻的奇迹` — wrong_behavior — `rules/cards/card-mygo/src/miracle.rs:30`
- Sheet: 「（2）[持续] 当你[经过]场上的所有玩家各一次，获得相当于场上奇迹水晶数与你最相近的玩家资金后三位的资金，然后此卡进入弃牌堆。」
- Code: `On::Action(Action::PassTile("actor == owner && card.placed", Some(all_others_passed), pass_player)),`
- Why: Entries in one run_hook call are admitted against the pre-call world (`let mut cur = world.clone()` at host.rs:1886, then `self.entry_admitted(world, ...)` at host.rs:1910) while only the bodies write `cur`; the record entry (line 29) and the reward entry (line 30) are separate entries, so all_others_passed never sees the bit record_pass just wrote. Probe zz_miracle_reward_fires_on_completing_pass fails: after the step that completes the set the card is still placed, money unchanged; the next pass (a later tile/turn) pays and discards (zz_miracle_reward_probe_next_step: money only becomes 10003 on step 2). The card therefore keeps its (1) discount past the exit, and the delayed fire can re-read a different crystal count for the closest-player pick.
- Fix: Do record and reward in one body (record the new bits, then test the completed mask and reward in the same handler), or otherwise make the guard see the current step's write.

### `MyGO:难以复刻的奇迹` — wrong_behavior — `rules/cards/card-mygo/src/miracle.rs:30`
- Sheet: 「当你[经过]场上的所有玩家各一次，获得相当于场上奇迹水晶数与你最相近的玩家资金后三位的资金，然后此卡进入弃牌堆。」
- Code: `    On::Action(Action::PassTile("actor == owner && card.placed", Some(all_others_passed), pass_player)),`
- Why: The reward's guard reads the mask written by the other PassTile entry in the same event. Guards are evaluated against the pre-event snapshot: `run_hook` passes the original `world` to `entry_admitted` (host.rs:1909) and the guard store is `world.clone()` (host.rs:1668), while only bodies advance `cur`, so `all_others_passed` cannot see the bit `record_pass` just wrote. The payout slips to the next PassTile — and because this entry's own condition has no `players_on(...) > 0`, that next tile may hold nobody. Probe: landing on the last unpassed player with dice 1 leaves the card on the field and pays 0; with the mask already complete, entering an empty tile pays the rival's last three digits. So the gain is one pass late and can be computed against the wrong moment/rival purse. RELATED backlog COND-01 (line 2477) describes the same guard-snapshot symptom for a FilterValue gate but does not name this card.
- Fix: Do the bookkeeping and the completion test in one entry (record the bits, then compute `all` including the current tile), or have `record_pass` raise the reward in its own body.

### `MyGO:（soyo）混合的颜色` — wrong_behavior — `rules/cards/card-mygo/src/soyo_colors.rs:35`
- Sheet: 「「因该效果从在其他颜色的地产商格子触发结算的玩家处收费时，收费在地产商的减半收费基础上额外减半。」」
- Code: `    if !ctx::trigger::pay_is_rent() {         return false;     }     if ctx::trigger::target() != player_id {         return false;     }     let t = ctx::trigger::tile(); ...     mine >= 0 && t == mine`
- Why: The guard set proves only: rent-shaped pay, payee is the card's controller, charged tile is the card's tile. Nothing tests that the charge arose from an agent settle at an other-colour agent tile, though the clause's base is the agent's half charge (「在地产商的减半收费基础上」) and COVERAGE #3 words it as 'charging via another colour's agent from that tile'. Probe `zz_soyo_review::soyo_plain_landing` measured a plain landing on 天文馆 with the card at 130 (p1=9870), while the control without the card paid 260 (p1=9740). No TODO at the site; BACKLOG C-Q14 only records the same-colour question, not this over-application.
- Fix: Also require the trigger to be an agent settle (e.g. from the pay trigger's agent tile / settle source), or carry the agent half-charge flag into the pay trigger; otherwise add TODO(规则书) at the guard.

### `MyGO:（soyo）混合的颜色` — wrong_behavior — `rules/cards/card-mygo/src/soyo_colors.rs:41` [recorded]
- Sheet: 「因该效果从在其他颜色的地产商格子触发结算的玩家处收费时，收费在地产商的减半收费基础上额外减半。」
- Code: `    let t = ctx::trigger::tile();     if t < 0 {         return false;     }     let Some(mine) = ctx::self_tile() else {         return false;     };     mine >= 0 && t == mine`
- Why: Verified: rules/tiles/src/agent.rs:85 is the only caller that charges the card's tile with the agent half flag (pay_rent(..., true)); rules/tiles/src/property.rs:78 charges 地租 with half=false on a plain landing. Agent tiles are price 0 (data/board.json: 主要街道 has price 0) so the card can only sit on a property tile, never on a 地产商 tile. on_rent only tests is_placed / pay_is_rent / payee==owner / tile==card tile, so the plain full-rent landing on the card's own deed is halved too, even though the sheet's base 「地产商的减半收费」 exists only in an agent settle. The same over-halving hits any card-driven rent (card_charge_rent, play.rs:2287-2299, explicitly 「收费」 not 「结算」).
- Fix: Gate on the settle having been triggered at an agent tile (the payer's position being a tile:agent / the trigger carrying the agent half) before halving.

### `MyGO:（乐奈）有趣的女人` — wrong_behavior — `rules/cards/card-mygo/src/rana_funny.rs:22`
- Sheet: 「当奇迹水晶总数为5或以上时使下一个经过的你以外的玩家选择失去一个“抹茶芭菲”或强制停下并[触发结算]」
- Code: `On::Action(Action::PassTile("card.placed && move.kind != Teleport && move.remaining > 0 && card.tile == tile.id", None, pass_tile)),`
- Why: Confirmed and reproduced. The pre is shared by both clauses and requires a mid-walk step, so the endpoint (remaining == 0, play.rs:1710/1743) and any teleport never reach the trap. The rulebook makes the move endpoint a [经过] (rulebook-doc.md:213 「移动终点触发[经过]」), and the trap sentence has no 「未在其上[触发结算]」 qualifier of its own (only the crystal sentence does), so a foreign landing at 5+ should get the choice, the half-pay settle and the card shuffle.
