Probe: P1 with a 抹茶芭菲 and 5 crystals landing exactly on the card's tile gets only the buy prompt, crystals stay 5, card stays on field. No TODO at the site.
- Fix: Fire the hook on `card.placed && card.tile == tile.id`; apply the 5+/foreign trap to every such event and gate only the crystal branch on a non-settling pass (`remaining > 0 || !ctx::move_resolve()`).

### `MyGO:（灯）不再迷茫` — extra_effect — `rules/cards/card-mygo/src/tomori_no_longer.rs:41`
- Sheet: 「你使用角色技能时可移除此卡上的一个[奇迹水晶]以代替此次技能的火罐消耗」
- Code: `On::Action(Actions::ChargePaying("actor == owner && card.counter('crystals') >= 1", None, cover)),`
- Why: ChargePaying is raised by fire::spend -> spend_pool (bandori-std/src/fire.rs:310-340), and fire::spend is the shared verb: character skills call it (tomori.rs:171) but so do card effects (layer_keep.rs:160, misaki_card.rs:69, tomori_inner_shout.rs:183) and the event 冲榜 (chart_rush.rs:31). The guard only checks the actor/owner and the crystal count, so a 冲榜 fire loss or a card-effect cost also prompts the substitution and burns this card's crystal. The spec's -03 trigger is "a character-skill press that costs 火罐". (The finding's example card names 层叠的守护 / 另一个我 are not literal card names in the tree, but the mechanism is exactly right.)
- Fix: Gate the offer on a character-skill-spend marker, or add TODO(规则书) naming the missing vocabulary.

### `MyGO:（灯）不再迷茫` — wrong_behavior — `rules/cards/card-mygo/src/tomori_no_longer.rs:103`
- Sheet: 「当此卡上的水晶由于此效果以外的原因减少时，此卡立刻获得等同于减少量的[奇迹水晶]」
- Code: `fn refill_guard(_player_id: i32) -> bool {     trigger::name() == card_sdk::abi::counter::CRYSTALS }`
- Why: CounterChanged is not an own-card kind: collect_hook_jobs (wasm_rules.rs:5248-5270) walks every placed instance of every player and collects a job for each card that declares the kind, then the pre is evaluated per hook card. refill's pre is only pre::MINE ("actor == owner", card_sdk/src/lib.rs:783) and the guard has neither trigger::card_is(ID) nor ctx::is_placed(), so a crystal change on ANY other card of the same owner passes and refill runs here; ctx::add_crystals is documented as "on **this card instance**" (bandori-std/src/lib.rs:194-207) and writes the running instance, so the other card's loss is refunded onto 不再迷茫. sweep_guard and every sibling crystal hook (that_day_rain, endless_journey, crimson_soul, nanami_effort) do carry card_is(ID). Worse with two copies of this card on the field: one copy's own -1 raises CounterChanged, both copies' refill hooks run and each consumes a SLOT_COVERING, and the second deferred raise then finds the slot at 0 and refunds both copies, so the skill-cost swap costs nothing.
- Fix: Add `trigger::card_is(ID)` and `ctx::is_placed()` to refill_guard (and keep the name check).

### `MyGO:（立希）想认真去做` — extra_effect — `rules/cards/card-mygo/src/taki_serious.rs:75`
- Sheet: 「本次结算导致的所有[支付]变为原价的四分之一」
- Code: `        ctx::plan::set_pay_factor(250);`
- Why: `plan::set_pay_factor` writes the persistent `turn.plan.pay_factor` (crates/game-core/src/engine/ops.rs:134-135) and nothing restores it. `turn.plan` is not reset between moves (play.rs:52-57); the next main move clones it (play.rs:1346) and `scale_settle_payment` reads `turn.plan.pay_factor` (play.rs:2503). Only `next_turn` replaces the whole TurnCtx (play.rs:552-556), so the leak is turn-scoped but covers the turn player's own main-move settle (and any later settle that turn) at a quarter price, though the book scopes the quarter to 「本次结算」. The file has no TODO(规则书).
- Fix: Restore the prior factor (or `set_pay_factor(1000)` after the loop), or add `TODO(规则书)（本次结算导致的所有[支付]…）: 减价只限本次结算 -- set_pay_factor 写 turn.plan 且不还原，后续主要移动也被四分之一`.

### `MyGO:（立希）想认真去做` — wrong_behavior — `rules/cards/card-mygo/src/taki_serious.rs:75`
- Sheet: 「本次结算导致的所有[支付]变为原价的四分之一」
- Code: `ctx::plan::set_pay_factor(250);`
- Why: Verified: the quarter is armed on the turn-wide move plan and never restored. TurnCtx.plan is only rebuilt at next_turn (play.rs:552) and main_move clones it (play.rs:1346); scale_settle_payment reads turn.plan.pay_factor at pay time (play.rs:2503). A test I ran (rules/tests/tests/zz_taki_leak.rs) shows the player's own 60-rent main-move landing afterwards pays 20. The factor also overwrites any factor an earlier card left. No TODO(规则书) at the site.
- Fix: Save turn.plan.pay_factor before the loop, restore it after the last settle.

### `PP:[冰川日菜]会发出怎样的声音呢？` — wrong_behavior — `rules/cards/card-pp/src/hina_sound.rs:45`
- Sheet: 「（1）此卡在符合使用条件时可替代丸山彩，大和麻弥，白鹭千圣，或若宫伊芙的专属卡。」
- Code: `const SUBS: [&str; 3] = [     "PP:[丸山彩]憧憬的前方",     "PP:[白鹭千圣]微笑的铁假面",     "PP:[若宫伊芙]属于我的武士道！", ];`
- Why: The cell (cards.json line 94) names four exclusive cards; SUBS holds three and the ask (line 61) is built only from SUBS, so 「PP:[大和麻弥]可能性为∞」 is never offered. Nothing else in the file offers it at play time -- 大和麻弥 occurs only in the doc comment (line 42) and in the clause-(2) swap TODO (line 85). Since the three offered subs have unconditioned [手] (aya_longing.rs:30, chisato_mask.rs:26, eve_bushido.rs:27 all On::Play("", None, _)), the sheet's 「在符合使用条件时」 gate also never refuses.
- Fix: Offer 麻弥 in the ask and place it with ctx::place_card rather than running a [手] it does not have (or at minimum add TODO(规则书)[特]（1）).

### `PP:[大和麻弥]可能性为∞` — wrong_behavior — `rules/cards/card-pp/src/infinite_possibility.rs:90`
- Sheet: 「（2）[共鸣]交换[拥有者]的抽卡区和弃卡区。」
- Code: `for c in &deck {         ctx::to_discard(player_id, c.as_str());     }     for c in &disc {         ctx::add_to_dest(player_id, c.as_str(), ctx::Dest::DeckTop);     }`
- Why: These are copies, not moves. `ctx::to_discard` only removes a copy from the hand and pushes the id into the graveyard (`World::to_discard`, ops.rs:1043-1055), so deck cards stay in the deck; `add_to_dest(DeckTop)` pushes into the deck without removing from the graveyard. Every card therefore ends up in BOTH piles: deck = old deck + old discard, discard = old discard + old deck. Probes: deck=[GREAT], discard=[PERFECT] (plus the 共鸣 fuel just filed) produced deck=[共鸣,PERFECT,GREAT] and discard=[PERFECT,共鸣,GREAT] -- 3 physical cards now in 6 slots; the first probe showed 2+2 cards becoming 5+5. The deck is never actually cleared and the discard never ends up holding the old deck alone. No swap test exists in spec_card_pp.rs and BACKLOG has no item for it.
- Fix: Remove each card from its source pile while moving it (take from deck/graveyard), or snapshot for logging, clear one pile, then move the other's cards in -- never copy.

### `PP:[大和麻弥]可能性为∞` — wrong_behavior — `rules/cards/card-pp/src/infinite_possibility.rs:91`
- Sheet: 「「（2）[共鸣]交换[拥有者]的抽卡区和弃卡区。」」
- Code: `let deck = ctx::cards_in(player_id, ctx::CardPile::Deck); let disc = ctx::cards_in(player_id, ctx::CardPile::Graveyard); for c in &deck {     ctx::to_discard(player_id, c.as_str()); } for c in &disc {     ctx::add_to_dest(player_id, c.as_str(), ctx::Dest::DeckTop); }`
- Why: Incorrect by construction: ctx::to_discard (wasm_rules.rs:1117 -> ops.rs:1043) removes a copy from the HAND only and pushes to the graveyard; Wall: add_to_dest with DeckTop (wasm_rules.rs:1059-1073) only pushes. The deck keeps its cards and gains the whole discard; the discard keeps its cards and gains the whole deck. Re-ran the probe on the live engine: before deck=[GREAT,10次招募] disc=[PERFECT,雨] -> after deck=[共鸣,雨,PERFECT,GREAT,10次招募] disc=[PERFECT,雨,共鸣,GREAT,10次招募]; 共鸣 ends in BOTH piles. Sibling resonance.rs shows the correct idiom (take_card then to_discard). No TODO(规则书) and no BACKLOG item covers the swap.
- Fix: take_card(player, c, CardPile::Deck) before to_discard and take_card(..., CardPile::Graveyard) before add_to_dest (or add a zone-swap ctx helper).

### `PP:[白鹭千圣]微笑的铁假面` — wrong_behavior — `rules/cards/card-pp/src/chisato_mask.rs:73`
- Sheet: 「（2）[共鸣]其他玩家[分摊][支付][拥有者]1500资金。」
- Code: `    if crate::resonance::try_resonance(player_id)? {`
- Why: The clause is under [持续] but the whole body lives in the [手] handler and runs once, right after the 2000 leg. The card's only Play entry is ungated and a placed non-`skill:` card has no press surface: view_extra_parts only pushes `skill:` field instances into MatchView.skills (crates/game-core/src/engine/mod.rs:1315-1317), and Side.tsx only sends `act: "play"` for hand cards (Side.tsx:398); play_from_hand itself requires the card in hand (play.rs:4687). FieldSheet opens field cards with NO_ACTIONS. So once the mask is placed, clause (2) can never fire again even if the owner later draws a 共鸣. The repo's idiom for a [持续] [共鸣] is a persistent offer; chisato_mask has neither a placed-gated press entry nor a hook stand-in.
- Fix: Give (2) its own persistent offer (a `card.placed`-gated press entry, or a hook like 再次闪耀's TurnStart stand-in) and keep the [手] body to place + the 2000 split.

### `PP:[衍生]共鸣` — wrong_behavior — `rules/cards/card-pp/src/resonance.rs:60`
- Sheet: 「将此卡放入弃卡区」
- Code: `if !ctx::take_card(player_id, RESONANCE.id, ctx::CardPile::Hand) {     return Ok(false); } // 「将此卡放入弃卡区」 ctx::to_discard(player_id, RESONANCE.id);`
- Why: Confirmed by execution and by the host path. ctx::take_card removes one copy (wasm_rules.rs:1081-1098, remove(k)); ctx::to_discard -> World::to_discard (ops.rs:1043-1054) independently does hand.iter().position(|c| c == card) and remove(k) before pushing to the graveyard. With two fuels both leave the hand but only one reaches the discard, so one card is destroyed. Probe run: BEFORE=[共鸣,共鸣,同一个梦想] AFTER=[] DISC0=[共鸣,同一个梦想] (zz_res_hand_probe.rs fails on fuel==1). The clause charges one copy. No TODO(规则书) and no BACKLOG/TEST-FINDINGS entry for this card.
- Fix: Use the single hand->discard primitive ctx::discard_from_hand(player_id, RESONANCE.id) as the cost (it removes one copy and pushes it), or drop take_card and rely on to_discard alone.

### `PP:[衍生]魔法战队Pastel✽Ranger` — wrong_behavior — `rules/cards/card-pp/src/ranger.rs:112`
- Sheet: 「4. 数量至少为5则获得1层状态“失去2000资金，下次盖房时减免2000（可溢出），盖房后减少1层”；」
- Code: `ctx::set_build_discount(2000, 1);`
- Why: Verified: `set_build_discount` writes only `world.turn.build_discount`/`build_discount_layers` (wasm_rules.rs:1861-1863), and `next_turn` replaces the whole turn ctx with `TurnCtx::default()` (play.rs:552-556; fields default 0 at world.rs:263-264). The status says 「下次盖房时」, not 「本回合」, and the layer wording 「盖房后减少1层」 only makes sense if it survives until a build; a ranger played without an immediate build loses the layer at end of turn. The sibling PP:练习生解密指南 banks the same status in the persistent player state `trainee_guide.buildOff` (trainee_guide.rs:187,203) and re-applies it in `before_build`, so it survives. No TODO/BACKLOG entry exists.
- Fix: Bank the layer in a persistent player state and apply it at buildBefore / pop it after the build, like trainee_guide does.

### `PP:不要背负期待` — missing_clause — `rules/cards/card-pp/src/no_expectation.rs:32`
- Sheet: 「「非回合开始时进行投掷的投掷结果减少2」」
- Code: `        On::Filter(Filter::DiceRollValue("card.placed", None, roll_shave)),`
- Why: The shave is registered only on DiceRollValue, and that stage is raised only inside a move (play.rs:890 card_move_seeded, :1415 main_move). Every card-driven effect roll goes through ctx::roll -> hostfns.rs:52-67 with window=true, which raises only diceRollBefore (wasm_rules.rs:4031-4035) and returns the face -- diceRollAfter is move-only too. The clause sentence has no �移动 roll restriction (the parenthetical 「如果是移动投掷则…」 exists precisely to carve the move-only floor/no-settle out of a general roll rule), so an owner's mid-turn 1d10/1d20/1d4 from another card is never reduced. Concrete: `skill:月岛麻里奈:礼物还有好多好多哟` has the owner roll 1d10 mid-turn (marina.rs:63); a 6 is unshaved and still clears the >=6 payout line, where the book makes it 4 and fails. Nothing in BACKLOG records this.
- Fix: Shave the face returned by ctx::roll for the owner (a roll-value stage on effect rolls), not only the move dispatch.

### `PP:不要背负期待` — wrong_behavior — `rules/cards/card-pp/src/no_expectation.rs:32`
- Sheet: 「非回合开始时进行投掷的投掷结果减少2」
- Code: `        On::Filter(Filter::DiceRollValue("card.placed", None, roll_shave)),`
- Why: DiceRollValue is raised only by the engine's own move walk (play.rs:890 card_move_seeded, play.rs:1415 main_move). `ctx::roll`/`ctx::roll_direct` (hence `ctx::do_move_roll` = card-driven 移动掷骰) go through HostRequest::Roll, which raises only `diceRollBefore` (wasm_rules.rs:4031) or nothing (roll_direct returns early when !window) — so no effect 投掷 during a turn and no card-driven move roll is ever shaved. The parenthetical only removes the 0 floor for non-move throws, so every non-turn-start throw should lose 2. No TODO at the site (the file has no TODO at all).
- Fix: Add TODO(规则书) and a shave on the ctx::roll / roll_direct path (carry purpose + at-turn-start so the move-only 0 floor and the turn-start exclusion stay conditional).

### `PP:初次演出事故` — extra_effect — `rules/cards/card-pp/src/first_live_accident.rs:50`
- Sheet: 「[拥有者]不可使用任何Pastel✽Palettes角色的 （2）技能。」
- Code: `ctx::set_tok(player_id, "skillBlock:Pastel✽Palettes", 1, 0, -1);`
- Why: The token is written with instance = -1, so its Counter row has instance None (ops.rs:2481-2499) and is player-held, not bound to this card instance. Every field-leave path clears only bound units: unplace_at -> destroy_instance_units(uid) retains tokens with `t.instance != Some(uid)` (ops.rs:1506-1509, 2114-2121), and unplace_card_named uses exactly that path (overlapping_voices.rs:71-75). Consequently, after the accident is banished (routinely, by 重叠的声音) the skillBlock token stays at 1 forever and PP (2) skills remain locked, while the [持续] should end with the card -- the C# reference the header cites is a dynamic `PlacedOf(player_id).Any(c => c.Live && c is CardFirstLiveAccident)`. skill_blocked reads the token before anything else (bandori-std/src/lib.rs:345-353), and only the third branch is field/mark based. No TODO or BACKLOG item records this.
- Fix: Capture place_card's returned uid and pass it as the instance argument: `let uid = ctx::place_card(...); ctx::set_tok(player_id, "skillBlock:Pastel✽Palettes", 1, 0, uid);`.

### `PP:初次演出事故` — missing_clause — `rules/cards/card-pp/src/first_live_accident.rs:50`
- Sheet: 「[拥有者]不可使用任何Pastel✽Palettes角色的（2）技能。」
- Code: `ctx::set_tok(player_id, "skillBlock:Pastel✽Palettes", 1, 0, -1);`
- Why: The sheet cell is exactly 「[拥有者]不可使用任何Pastel✽Palettes角色的（2）技能。」 The whole [持续] is reduced to a token, but only aya.rs:60/93 (丸山彩), chisato.rs:44 (白鹭千圣) and eve.rs:82 (若宫伊芙) call skill_blocked with "Pastel✽Palettes". maya.rs on_draw (registered at maya.rs:33, 「可选择」 so it prompts) and hina.rs roll (registered at hina.rs:41, rolls 1d4 and places a borrowed skill) never consult the token. There is no generic dispatch gate: skill_blocked lives in bandori-std, which the engine cannot call, and the aya/eve comments call these residual guards, so the header's 'shared skill-press gate reads' it claim is false for two of the five P✽P (2) skills. Because the accident's own [手] draws at line 60 after the token is set, 大和麻弥's prompt can appear immediately from playing this card. No TODO(规则书) exists in the file; BACKLOG SKILL-01 / TEST-FINDINGS C-PP-first-accident-02 name only the already-fixed 丸山彩 hook, so maya/hina are unrecorded.
- Fix: Have maya's on_draw gate on bandori_std::ctx::skill_blocked(player_id, "Pastel✽Palettes") and have hina's roll skip the roll (and borrow) while blocked, as aya/chisato/eve already do.

### `PP:可爱又强壮的花朵` — wrong_behavior — `rules/cards/card-pp/src/strong_flower.rs:110`
- Sheet: 「1. [强制停下]，此次移动变为[结算]；」
- Code: `plan::set_stop_at(tile);     plan::set_resolve(true);`
- Why: [强制停下] is an [异常移动效果] (glossary 15) and [不可阻挡] lets the affected player choose whether such an effect applies (glossary 14). ctx::gate(...abnormal::Stop) is the host entry that runs that abnormal chain (wasm_rules.rs:3752 -> abnormal_gate, so 安可 / 像往常一样 get their window); every sibling forced-stop pass hook calls it first (rana_funny.rs:107, kasumi_love_all.rs:78, council_check.rs:134, before_live.rs:44, kaoru_thief.rs:123, hagumi_marks.rs:143). Here neither the stop, the 1500 gain, nor the discard can be refused or countered. CROSS-TESTS L2 pins the intended outcome: answering the stop voids the stop 'and everything it would cause' and the card stays on the tile; M14 variant b likewise keeps the card. Confirmed.
- Fix: Before line 110 add `if !ctx::gate(trigger::player_id(), bandori_std::kind::abnormal::Stop) { return Ok(()); }` (as kasumi_love_all.rs:78 does) so refusal voids the whole [持续].

### `PP:练习生解密指南` — wrong_behavior — `rules/cards/card-pp/src/trainee_guide.rs:147`
- Sheet: 「（2）回合结束时添加1个[奇迹水晶]。」
- Code: `fn turn_end_guard(player_id: i32) -> bool {     ctx::is_placed() }`
- Why: Engine turnEnd is dispatched over every player's field (wasm_rules.rs:5005-5042 `for player_id in 0..world.player_count()`), the guard's player_id is the card holder while trigger.player_id is the ending seat, and this guard never looks at either. The card therefore gains a crystal (and can cross 5 and cash in) at every opponent's turn end too.
- Fix: Use `Action::TurnEnd(card_sdk::pre::MINE, None, turn_end)` (pre::MINE is the documented `actor == owner` shorthand) or add `ctx::trigger::player_id() == player_id` to the guard.

### `PP:练习生解密指南` — wrong_behavior — `rules/cards/card-pp/src/trainee_guide.rs:202` [recorded]
- Sheet: 「每个单色效果为获得1层状态“下次盖房的价格减少1000（可溢出），盖房后减少1层”；」
- Code: `fn before_build(player_id: i32) -> card_sdk::Asked {     let n = ctx::state::get(player_id, "trainee_guide.buildOff");     if n <= 0 {         return Ok(());     }     ctx::set_build_discount(1000 * n, 1);`
- Why: cash_in adds the buildOff state and then sends the card to the Graveyard (line 196). BuildBefore/BuildAfter are On::Action hooks of that instance and hook dispatch only walks `world.field_instances` (wasm_rules.rs:5005-5042, plus turn-scoped lingering); a discarded card is never walked. (3) always discards, so before_build/after_build can never fire again: the mono payout exists only as an orphaned player state, and after_build cannot peel it either. Reproduced: play with 15 fans (5 crystals -> instant cash-in), own a tile and build -> house cost 2000 fully spent, buildOff stays 1. Forcing the state while the card is still placed does work (spends 0 then 1000), so only the instance lifetime is broken.
- Fix: Move the discount off the card instance onto a persistent listener (a `status:` rule gated on the player's buildOff state with its own BuildBefore/BuildAfter), the way other player-scoped statuses are hosted.

### `PPP:Returns` — wrong_behavior — `rules/cards/card-ppp/src/returns.rs:421`
- Sheet: 「如果和当前因此卡获得的团卡不一样则替换并移除上面的所有[奇迹水晶]。」
- Code: `    if prev >= 0 {         if let Some(old) = SKILLS.get(prev as usize) {             ctx::unplace_card_named(player_id, old);`
- Why: A Returns owner is necessarily a Poppin' Party character, so `bind_skills` (engine/setup.rs:134 -> ops.rs:1540-1544, at persona pick, long before the deckAtGameStart hook) has already placed the owner's own bound `skill:Poppin' Party:星之鼓动`. When the previous borrow is also Poppin' Party (any other PPP survivor), the replacement names the same id, and `ctx::unplace_card_named` -> `World::unplace_card` (game-core/engine/ops.rs:1781) takes the FIRST field entry with `position`, i.e. the owner's own card, leaving the borrowed copy. The own instance's crystals die with it (destroy_instance_units) even though the book removes the card the effect obtained. Confirmed by scratch probe: after the start borrow field had two identical PPP skills (uids 75 = own, 76 = borrowed); after choosing Pastel, uid 75 was gone and uid 76 remained. Not in BACKLOG (only LOY-RET-01/02/03) or TEST-FINDINGS.
- Fix: Remember the borrowed instance uid and `ctx::unplace_at(...)` it (keeping the uid across the same-band early return) instead of unplacing by name.

### `PPP:Returns` — wrong_behavior — `rules/cards/card-ppp/src/returns.rs:422`
- Sheet: 「如果和当前因此卡获得的团卡不一样则替换并移除上面的所有[奇迹水晶]」
- Code: `ctx::unplace_card_named(player_id, old);`
- Why: Confirmed. World::unplace_card (crates/game-core/src/engine/ops.rs:1779-1786) removes the FIRST field entry whose card string matches. The owner's own PPP band card is the same skill id and is placed earlier by bind_skills (ops.rs:1530-1545, at setup), so when the band being swapped out is Poppin' Party the own card is destroyed and the borrowed copy survives; crystal removal (destroy_instance_units, ops.rs:2114-2122) also runs on the wrong uid. Reproduced live on this tree with rules/tests/tests/zz_returns_probe.rs: field after start [own PPP(uid2), Returns, borrowed PPP(uid76)], after swapping that borrow to Pastel the uids are [1,75,76,79,81] -- uid 2 (own) gone, uid 76 (borrowed) still live. Comments at 239-240/356-359/416-419 assert the opposite. No TODO or BACKLOG entry.
- Fix: Unplace by the recorded instance uid (`returns.borrow_uid`) instead of by card name (needs an unplace-by-uid ABI).

### `PPP:STAR BEAT!` — wrong_behavior — `rules/cards/card-ppp/src/star_beat.rs:22` [recorded]
- Sheet: 「下次结算后可选择在绝对距离5格以内自己拥有的格子上进行一次盖房，随后减少1层」
- Code: `On::Action(Action::SettleAfter("actor == owner && slot('star_beat.layers') > 0", Some(has_near_buildable), after_settle)),`
- Why: Confirmed. Hook dispatch for a non-game-start trigger runs only over placed field instances, board/tile/event/mark/status, and turn lingering (crates/game-rules/src/wasm_rules.rs:5005-5167); the only own-card hand paths are Drawn/Discarded (4984). A hand play has no instance (wasm_rules.rs:9306-9312, uid -1) and `play` never calls set_dest(Dest::Field)+place_card nor ctx::linger, so the played card lands in the graveyard (play.rs:4764-4794) and `after_settle` never runs. Independent probe: giving and playing the card, landing on an owned buildable tile with money 20000, offered only ask.buy; with a layer preset and the card hand-held, a normal roll-settle also only offered ask.build, never star_beat_title. With the card pre-placed the same settle does offer star_beat_title, so placement is the carrier. Not in BACKLOG/TEST-FINDINGS. Sibling [手] cards that need a carrier place themselves (card-sumimi/two_donuts.rs:67-70, card-ppp/arisa_wait.rs:47).
- Fix: Carry the hook on a host instance (set_dest(Dest::Field)+place_card, or ctx::linger/status) so a later settle can offer the build.

### `PPP:STAR BEAT!` — wrong_behavior — `rules/cards/card-ppp/src/star_beat.rs:22`
- Sheet: 「[使用者]获得1层状态“下次结算后可选择在绝对距离5格以内自己拥有的格子上进行一次盖房，随后减少1层”」
- Code: `        On::Action(Action::SettleAfter("actor == owner && slot('star_beat.layers') > 0", Some(has_near_buildable), after_settle)),`
- Why: `play` (line 75-76) grants the slot but never sets a Dest, so the played card lands in the graveyard (`Dest` unset -> `dest_from` wildcard -> Graveyard, documented at wasm_rules.rs:154-156). SettleAfter dispatch walks only field / board / lingering instances, so after_settle is unreachable; the player slot survives but has no dispatcher and is inert forever.
- Fix: Have `play` place its own copy on the field (ctx::place_self_to_field) so the SettleAfter entry is dispatched.

### `PPP:[衍生]拍卖撤下来了` — wrong_behavior — `rules/cards/card-ppp/src/auction_pulled.rs:23`
- Sheet: 「此卡加入手牌时将此卡放置在自身场上」
- Code: `&[On::Action(Action::Drawn("", None, hook)),`
- Why: The printed trigger is 「加入手牌时」 (any way into a hand), but the only entry is `Action::Drawn`, and the engine raises `drawn` solely from the draw routine (play.rs:4537) plus the `DrewBefore` replacement branch in `add_to_dest` (wasm_rules.rs:1047-1055); any other add-to-hand is a bare push there. A concrete reachable route exists: 相信你's 「从你的卡组中选择两张加入你的手牌」 adds deck picks with `ctx::add_to_dest(..., Dest::Hand)` (card-hhw/src/believe_you.rs:101-103), so an auction copy still sitting at the deck bottom would land in hand and never place. The card has no `On::Play`, so that copy is also unplayable until a Random Star's sweep shuffles it back. The repo contract C-PPP-auction-off-01 pins exactly this trigger ("draw / deal / any add-to-hand", ppp.md:444) and names `auction_off_places_itself_on_hand_add` (ppp.md:450), but only `auction_off_raises_fire_cap` exists. No TODO and no BACKLOG/TEST-FINDINGS entry.
- Fix: Add a hand-entry point (or hook the add-to-hand commit) and keep `Drawn` as the draw half; add the contract's missing test.

### `PPP:迷宫般的仓库` — wrong_behavior — `rules/cards/card-ppp/src/maze_warehouse.rs:47`
- Sheet: 「如果购买则拆除那个格子上的所有房屋」
- Code: `fn raze(_player_id: i32, mut v: DealValue) -> DealValue {     if trigger::tile() < 0 {         return v;     }     v.houses = Some(0);     v }`
- Why: The clause is verbatim in the cell and the hook is declared (On::Filter(Filter::BuyAssign(pre::MINE, None, raze)), line 22); pre::MINE is "actor == owner" and CardInfo::hooks() includes On::Filter (crates/game-rules/src/host.rs:202,215-224), so the fold runs and answers Some(0). But the wasm counteract write-back (crates/game-rules/src/wasm_rules.rs:5229-5240 and the identical resume tail at 9591-9602) copies back only value/negation/spared/target, never trigger.buy, so the engine's `at.buy` at crates/game-core/src/engine/play.rs:2938-2945 still holds deal_houses Some(3) and assign_deed keeps the houses. Independently reproduced: the existing scratch probe rules/tests/tests/zz_maze_review.rs (tile 48 山吹面包房, 3 houses, 1d10=2) prints `after buy: pos=48 owner=Some(0) house=3 money=100` and `after skip: owner=Some(0) house=3 money=100` -- the free half works (price 0) but the raze never lands, and the counteraction point itself did fire (`DBG counteract kind=buyAssign`). This card is the only BuyAssign user, so the clause has no working path (`at.buy` is also identical in the force/acq/auction paths at 1217/2619/4408) and there is no TODO at the site.
- Fix: Carry the fold back: `t.buy = trigger.buy` at the end of counteract_orig and at the resume tail of counteract, so the BuyAssign deal rewrite reaches assign_deed; the card's hook and Some(0) are otherwise correct.

### `PPP:（里美）我的心就像巧克力螺` — wrong_behavior — `rules/cards/card-ppp/src/rimi_choco.rs:31`
- Sheet: 「期间[不可阻挡]。」
- Code: `    rules_status::move_blocked(player_id)`
- Why: cant_play is the whole gate, so a [停留] player can never play the card: probe with stay=1 + skip_move returns Err("status.move_blocked"). wasm_rules::cant_move returns 3 whenever st.skip_move is latched (wasm_rules.rs:2145-2147), and a [停留] grant latches exactly that (bandori-std/status.rs:125 skip_main_move(after>0 && !unstoppable)). The card's own clause makes this walk [不可阻挡], which glossary 51 defines as 「无视已存在的[停留]」; card_move never re-reads the latch and the walk clamp (play.rs:1461) reads Gate::Move, which the state grant at line 60 opens — so only the guard stands in the way. This also refuses a player who already holds [不可阻挡] from another source, which to_the_peak.rs:76-83 explicitly admits. Note a stun/exile latch is safe to admit too: those close the Act gate first, which play.rs:4620 refuses before the rule guard (and this card has no PLAYABLE_STUNNED exemption). No TODO(规则书), no BACKLOG entry.
- Fix: Mirror to_the_peak: when the only reason is a [停留] latch (stay>0, no blocking stun/exile), let the play through — the card itself grants [不可阻挡] before the walk — while still refusing not-your-turn / already-moved.

### `RAS:EXIST` — wrong_behavior — `rules/cards/card-ras/src/exist.rs:19`
- Sheet: 「「场上及打出的所有对单一玩家生效的手卡（包括其他玩家指向自身的卡）的目标将改为你」」
- Code: `        On::Filter(Filter::Redirect("target != owner", None, redirect)),`
- Why: The parenthetical case is unimplemented. wasm_rules `target_player` returns at `if p == by { return Ok(p); }` (crates/game-rules/src/wasm_rules.rs:5621) before the `raise_core("redirect", ...)` at 5639, so another player's self-targeted single-target card never reaches EXIST. Reproduced: `cargo test -p ruleset-tests --test zz_exist_review probe_self_target_other_player -- --nocapture` -> P0 plays 欢迎来到ave mujica的世界 choosing itself; P0 skillState 1->2, EXIST holder P1 stays 1, `redirected log = false`. No TODO(规则书) at the site.
- Fix: In target_player raise/fold Redirect before the `p == by` early-out (engine), and add `TODO(规则书)（…）: …` at this hook for the until-fixed gap.

### `RAS:EXIST` — wrong_behavior — `rules/cards/card-ras/src/exist.rs:54`
- Sheet: 「场上及打出的所有对单一玩家生效的手卡（包括其他玩家指向自身的卡）的目标将改为你」
- Code: `/// single-target play is retargeted at this seat (「包括其他玩家指向自身的卡」 /// covers another player's self-targeted plays too). The engine only raises`
- Why: Real. `target_player` returns at `if p == by { return Ok(p); }` (crates/game-rules/src/wasm_rules.rs:5621-5623) before the `redirect` raise at :5639, so another player's self-targeted single-target play never reaches EXIST's filter. My run of rules/tests/tests/zz_exist_review.rs `probe_self_target_other_player` reproduced it: P0 played Mujica:欢迎来到ave mujica的世界 at itself with EXIST on P1's field; P0 skillState 1→2, P1 stayed 1, no redirect log. The doc comment claims the parenthetical is covered.
- Fix: Raise `redirect` for a self-targeted play by another player (move the `p == by` early-out below the Redirect raise, or re-raise for self targets) and pin it with a test.

### `RAS:Repaint` — wrong_behavior — `rules/cards/card-ras/src/repaint.rs:39`
- Sheet: 「X为对方原本预计路径上你拥有的格子数」
- Code: `let t = ctx::tile_steps_ahead(them, i);`
- Why: Verified: `tile_steps_ahead` (crates/game-core/src/engine/ops.rs:366-373) adds `steps` to the position with no direction factor, and the CEL pre's `on_path` (crates/cond/src/view_impl.rs:386-400) does `((pos + i) % n + n) % n` too, so both sites count strictly forward. The main move really can walk backwards: 美竹兰's 叛逆的红挑染 sets `plan::set_reverse(true)` in its DiceRollPlan window (`rules/skills/skill-personas/src/ran.rs:95`, i.e. before the dice), the walk loop steps by `m.dir()` = -1 when `reverse` (move_ctx.rs:284-290, play.rs:1704), and the positive roll still raises the diceRollResult window. On such a move the holder's deeds behind the mover are invisible to the pre (card never offered) and, if offered, X counts the wrong tiles. `tsugumi.rs:113`, `this_hand.rs:43`, `rimi_choco.rs:69`, `cant_look_away.rs:94`, `saki_move.rs:60`, `anon_tokyo.rs:160`, `detour.rs:198` also reverse. No TODO at the site.
- Fix: Walk `tile_steps_ahead(them, dir * i)` with the move/plan direction, and make the pre direction-aware instead of the forward-only `on_path` builtin.

### `RAS:游击演出` — wrong_behavior — `rules/cards/card-ras/src/guerrilla.rs:157`
- Sheet: 「此卡进入弃卡区的回合结束时」
- Code: `if ctx::turn_player() == player_id && ctx::slot(player_id, SLOT_ARMED) == 0 {`
- Why: The [特] is keyed to the end of the turn in which the card entered the discard, whoever's turn that is. `before_turn_end` -> `schedule_turn_end(player_id, 2, ..)` (ctx.rs:2900-2902); mode 2 sets `next_of_player = false` (hostfns.rs:2923), and the host stores `target = now` -- the current turn player -- regardless of the `player_id` argument (wasm_rules.rs:2283-2290). So the `turn_player() == player_id` test is redundant and actively blocks every off-turn discard. Verified empirically: the reviewer's probe `zz_guerrilla_probe.rs::probe_offturn_discard` (card discarded from p0's hand on p1's turn) reports prompt=None at p1's turn end; with only this guard removed and the ruleset rebuilt, the same probe prompts `cards:card-ras.guerrilla_title` at p1's turn end. Worse, when a played counteraction is filed to the graveyard the engine raises `discarded` (wasm_rules.rs:6345), so this hook -- not the manual arm at line 147 -- is the site that runs on the card's primary path, and the intended path (counteract during an opponent's turn) never revives. Raising severity from medium to high for that reason; line 147 carries the same gate and must be fixed too.
- Fix: Drop `ctx::turn_player() == player_id` at both sites (147 and 157) and arm on the SLOT_ARMED latch alone.

### `RAS:练习室里的风暴` — wrong_behavior — `rules/cards/card-ras/src/studio_storm.rs:55`
- Sheet: 「（1）当你位于你拥有的livehouse格子上时可将此卡放置在当前格子上」
- Code: `&& LIVEHOUSE_PROPS.iter().any(|&n| ctx::tile_named(n) == pos)`
- Why: The play gate accepts only the four house-holding props (const at 42-47). The repo's livehouse answer is `tile_in(_, t, "livehouse")` = buyable && group 6 (`rules/bandori-std/src/kind.rs` class VENUE / VENUE_GROUP, `is_live_house_for` in lib.rs:183), which also covers RiNG 1-4 (board.json indexes 9/17/37/54: kind ring, group 6, price 1000) -- and the same-set sibling `please_choose.rs:41-48` lists all four RiNG as livehouse tiles, while `be_strongest` maps all 8 buyable livehouses. The per-player ExtraColor designation (`band-skill 「视为live house格子」`, honored by `is_live_house_for`) is missed too. A play on an owned RiNG livehouse is therefore wrongly refused. `charge_rent` -> `rent_figure` even rolls the RiNG dice-rent, so the card was expected to sit there. No TODO at the site and nothing in BACKLOG.
- Fix: Keep the `tile_owner(pos) == player_id` half and replace the name list with `ctx::is_live_house_for(player_id, pos)`; cite 「livehouse格子」 on that line.

### `RAS:（和奏瑞依）寄于指尖的执念` — missing_clause — `rules/cards/card-ras/src/layer_keep.rs:100`
- Sheet: 「[反击] 当你使用火罐进行掷骰时，可打出此卡并保留（写下）未被选择的另一个骰点」
- Code: `fn counter_fire(player_id: i32) -> card_sdk::Asked {     ctx::log(player_id, &Msg::new(key!("layer_keep_counter")));     Ok(()) }`
- Why: The engine dispatches On::Counteract to counter_fire, never to play(); counter_fire only logs. It never calls ctx::set_dest(Dest::Field)/place_card, so DEST_UNSET flows to dest_from(-1) => Graveyard and the card is buried (wasm_rules.rs:6336-6352, dest_from at 9714). Probe zz_layer_keep_probe: after a real fire-pot counteraction field=[skills only], hand=[], discard=[the card], count=0; the card is then reshuffled back. Black_suits.rs places itself in its counteract body for the same reason.
- Fix: Place the card in counter_fire as black_suits.rs does: if !ctx::is_placed() { ctx::set_dest(Dest::Field); ctx::place_card(...) }.

### `RAS:（和奏瑞依）寄于指尖的执念` — missing_clause — `rules/cards/card-ras/src/layer_keep.rs:108`
- Sheet: 「可打出此卡并保留（写下）未被选择的另一个骰点」
- Code: `let unused = ctx::slot(player_id, SLOT_UNUSED);     if unused <= 0 {         return Ok(());     }`
- Why: Nobody writes layerUnused: `grep -rn layerUnused` hits only this file plus a webui state-label list. The fire-pot reroll (skill-personas/src/layer.rs:78) spends and rolls but writes no slot. roll_after therefore always early-returns. Probe zz_layer_keep_probe2 (card placed, real fire reroll, keep-old answered): count=0. Seeding layerUnused by hand makes it work (probe7: count=1), so the reader is fine and only the writer is missing.
- Fix: Write the rejected face+1 to layerUnused from layer after the 择其一 pick (or have this card capture both faces).

### `RAS:（和奏瑞依）寄于指尖的执念` — missing_todo — `rules/cards/card-ras/src/layer_keep.rs:101`
- Sheet: 「可打出此卡并保留（写下）未被选择的另一个骰点」
- Code: `ctx::log(player_id, &Msg::new(key!("layer_keep_counter")));`
- Why: The file has no TODO(规则书) at all (`grep -n TODO` empty), so the bury-on-counteract, the missing layerUnused writer, the value-based deletion, the 8-slot cap and the free hand play are all unrecorded. BACKLOG mentions the card only in LOY-DISP-02 (closed .text() quote fix), HOST-11 (slot encoding) and STATE-INIT-01 (a zero-seed nit).
- Fix: Add TODO(规则书) at counter_fire: the book's counteract play places the card and writes the unchosen die; the body only logs.

### `RAS:（和奏瑞依）寄于指尖的执念` — missing_todo — `rules/cards/card-ras/src/layer_keep.rs:108`
- Sheet: 「保留（写下）未被选择的另一个骰点」
- Code: `    let unused = ctx::slot(player_id, SLOT_UNUSED);`
- Why: The phantom-slot read has no TODO(规则书); BACKLOG STATE-INIT-01 only records the 0-default sentinel at line 3087, not that nothing ever produces a value.
- Fix: Add `TODO(规则书)（保留未被选择的另一个骰点）: the fire reroll never writes layerUnused -- nothing is banked`.

### `RAS:（和奏瑞依）寄于指尖的执念` — wrong_behavior — `rules/cards/card-ras/src/layer_keep.rs:24`
- Sheet: 「保留（写下）未被选择的另一个骰点」
- Code: `On::Action(Action::DiceRollAfter("actor == owner && card.placed", None, roll_after)),`
- Why: The keep hook is on the outer diceRollAfter and cannot line up with the counteraction play. The fire reroll is a nested ctx::roll (layer.rs:78) that raises only diceRollBefore, and the counteract window opens and resolves *before* 择其一执行; the card is placed during that nested window while the enclosing diceRollAfter hook walk iterates a world snapshot taken earlier (wasm_rules.rs:5006), so the freshly placed card is never visited. Probe8 with a temporary placement in counter_fire: card sits on the field, layerUnused=7 seeded, count=0 and no layer_keep_kept — the hook never ran.
- Fix: Do not rely on the outer DiceRollAfter walk: record the unchosen face from the reroll body after the pick (or re-raise the keep point after placement).

### `RAS:（和奏瑞依）寄于指尖的执念` — wrong_behavior — `rules/cards/card-ras/src/layer_keep.rs:28`
- Sheet: 「可保留多个骰点」
- Code: `        On::Counteract(             &[ChainKind::DiceRollBefore],             "actor == owner && roll_source == 1",`
- Why: Only hand cards are offered as counters unless the card carries prop COUNTERACT_FROM_FIELD (has_field_counter wasm_rules.rs:6584, declare_one:6723; tae.rs:60 and sayo.rs:55 set it). This CardDef has no `.props(...)` at all, so once the card is placed (the fixed placement of the counter body) it is never offered on a later fire reroll. The second and following dice can never be banked: 「可保留多个骰点」 is unreachable.
- Fix: Add `.props(&[(card_sdk::abi::prop::COUNTERACT_FROM_FIELD, 1)])`; consider `COUNTERACT_GROUP` as tae does so the placed card cannot re-offer itself in the same round.

### `RAS:（和奏瑞依）寄于指尖的执念` — wrong_behavior — `rules/cards/card-ras/src/layer_keep.rs:100`
- Sheet: 「可打出此卡并保留（写下）未被选择的另一个骰点」
- Code: `fn counter_fire(player_id: i32) -> card_sdk::Asked {     ctx::log(player_id, &Msg::new(key!("layer_keep_counter")));     Ok(())`
- Why: A counteraction declaration dispatches only the Counteract entry (host.rs:1539 `Call::Counteract => self.counteract_entry`), never the play body that holds `set_dest(Field)`+`place_card`. `counter_fire` only logs, so `dest` stays DEST_UNSET and dest_from maps it to Graveyard. The generic play is rejected in the window (`t.play` -> Err("err.play_phase"), play.rs:4667). even_lost::counteract does exactly this placement in its counter body.
- Fix: Do set_dest(Field)+place_card in counter_fire (as even_lost does) and keep `play` for the press branch.

### `R:NFO` — wrong_behavior — `rules/cards/card-roselia/src/nfo.rs:24`
- Sheet: 「若结果为5，为自己的角色卡添加6个奇迹水晶，你每次获得资金时，可消耗一个奇迹水晶使本次的额度提高300」
- Code: `On::Action(Action::Effect("", Some(gain_guard), gain_bump)),`
- Why: The +300 offer lives on the NFO card def, but nothing keeps NFO in play after result 5: the card is played from hand and, with no `set_dest`, the play lands in Graveyard. The Effect hook is only dispatched to field instances / lingering carries (`is_own_card_kind` is `Drawn` only, wasm_rules.rs:9697-9701; the Effect walk is field_instances + lingering, 5246-5369). So the 6 crystals survive as a name-keyed player counter while the only thing that reads them is gone. Probe on the built ruleset: forced 5, field empty, counter `cards:card-roselia.nfo_crystals`=6; play 通用:GREAT (+2000) -> 2000 moved, no ask, crystals unchanged. The line-175 comment sells this as a live attachment. Also true for the result-6 leg once its shave-payment discards the card.
- Fix: Keep a hook source alive while nfo_crystals > 0 (self-place/linger NFO), and say so in the comment.

### `R:向着顶点` — wrong_behavior — `rules/cards/card-roselia/src/to_the_peak.rs:40`
- Sheet: 「若所有livehouse格子已被购买，可花费1.5倍价格为属于你的一个livehouse格子加盖一层房屋。」
- Code: `fn why_not_build_on(player_id: i32, t: i32) -> Option<&'static str> {`
- Why: The local gate copies only own/ring/mortgaged/full/can_pay. ops.rs:1077-1135 additionally refuses an active prop::NO_BUILD (own field, board-tile and lingering instances) and prop::NO_BUILD_ABOVE (卡池BUG), and card-sdk ctx::can_build_on (ctx.rs:3425 -> hostfns.rs:1775) exposes exactly that world gate plus the payer gate. Today 卡池BUG (rules/events/src/pool_bug.rs:48, NO_BUILD_ABOVE=1500) and a placed 梦在前方，结彩当下 (dream_ahead.rs:62, NO_BUILD) are ignored, so the card raises houses the engine would refuse.
- Fix: Use ctx::can_build_on (own + tile gate + pay gate) in both cant_play and play.

### `R:选择自己的舞台` — missing_clause — `rules/cards/card-roselia/src/own_stage.rs:69`
- Sheet: 「（或无法移动的回合结束时）」
- Code: `ctx::set_slot(player_id, "stageEnd", if yes { 1 } else { 2 });`
- Why: The parenthetical makes the card also choose, at the end of a turn whose move an abnormal prevented ([停留]/[晕眩]), whether the standing tile settles. The code only parks a `stageEnd` flag, and no consumer exists (grep finds `stageEnd` only in own_stage.rs). `end_turn` (play.rs:721-762) raises turnEndBefore/status wear-off/turnEnd/turnEndAfter/endTurnAfter and never runs a landing settle; `begin_turn` only logs the move-gate skip (`log.stay_skip`). Probe: a stay-locked turn standing on an unowned Livehouse produced `LOCKED-END prompts=0` and only keys `log.stay_skip`, `log.end_turn`, `log.turn` at the end.
- Fix: Consume `stageEnd` at turn end when the main move was blocked and settle the standing tile when it reads 1, then clear it.

### `R:选择自己的舞台` — wrong_behavior — `rules/cards/card-roselia/src/own_stage.rs:74`
- Sheet: 「选择自己的本次移动（或无法移动的回合结束时）是否触发结算」
- Code: `ctx::set_slot(player_id, "resolveOverride", if yes { 1 } else { 0 });`
- Why: The card's whole payload is the yes/no gate on this move's settle, but the answer is only written to the player-state slot `resolveOverride` (and the log). A repo-wide grep for `resolveOverride`/`resolve_override`/`RESOLVE_OVERRIDE` finds no reader outside this file, while the engine's real settle gate is the in-flight plan's `resolve` (`ctx::plan::set_resolve` -> `play.rs:1766` syncs `plan.resolve` into the walk), which this card never touches. Probe `zz_own_stage_probe`: 蓝玫瑰的骄傲 forced [传送] onto an unowned Livehouse gives exactly one buy prompt with NO answer, with YES, and with no counteraction at all (retried here: `BASE pos=8 prompts=1`, `YES ... prompts=1`, `NO ... prompts=1`), so playing the card changes nothing.
- Fix: Have the counteraction write the in-flight move plan's resolve (the window runs inside `abnormal_gate`, after the effect shaped the plan and before `card_move` walks it), so a NO suppresses the landing settle.

### `R:（ykn）louder` — wrong_behavior — `rules/cards/card-roselia/src/louder.rs:25`
- Sheet: 「每次打出此卡时使ring的价格基础乘数+5」
- Code: `    let now = ctx::add_ring_bonus(5);`
- Why: The +5 only lands on the `rentUnitMult` rule-param bonus (`ctx::add_ring_bonus` -> `add_rent_unit_bonus` -> `add_rule_param_bonus("rentUnitMult", n)`, crates/game-core/src/engine/ops.rs:2438-2440). Every RiNG rent charge and rent quote is computed from the ring tile's static prop `rentDiceMult` instead (ops.rs:309-311 `rent_of`; play.rs:2371-2377 `rent_figure`; purchase.rs:544-548 `rent_quote`), stamped once at rules/tiles/src/ring.rs:42. No charge/quote path calls `rent_unit_mult()`; the slot is read only by the two Sumimi cards' PriceTag helper. So playing louder leaves every charged amount and every displayed rent unchanged.
- Fix: Have the charge/quote path fold `rent_unit_mult()` into the ring multiplier (or have the effect also write the tile's `rentDiceMult` prop), and record the gap at this site.

### `R:（亚子）黑暗大魔姬亚子` — wrong_behavior — `rules/cards/card-roselia/src/ako_dark.rs:46`
- Sheet: 「若此卡从手牌以外的地方打出，你抽一张卡」
- Code: `    if !ctx::play_from_hand() {`
- Why: Verified by running rules/tests/tests/zz_ako_dark_probe.rs: countering a tile-rent payment from hand leaves the same hand size (4->4) and shrinks the deck (8->7), i.e. the card wrongly draws. The flag is only raised inside Game::play_from_hand (crates/game-core/src/engine/play.rs:4707-4708); hand_counteractions (crates/game-rules/src/wasm_rules.rs:6054-6061) removes the declared card from hand and calls Call::Counteract without touching turn.play_from_hand, and the counter body drive at 6304 neither sets nor restores it. So for a counteraction the flag carries the answered activation's origin, not this card's.
- Fix: Record the declared card's own origin at declaration (true for a hand declaration, false for a placed source) and add both-direction tests.

### `R:（亚子）黑暗大魔姬亚子` — wrong_behavior — `rules/cards/card-roselia/src/ako_dark.rs:46`
- Sheet: 「若此卡从手牌以外的地方打出，你抽一张卡。」
- Code: `    if !ctx::play_from_hand() {         ctx::draw(player_id, 1)?;`
- Why: The counter body is driven with Call::Counteract through drive()/drive_inner_body_from(), which never sets turn.play_from_hand; only game-core play_from_hand() (play.rs:4707-4820) sets and scopes it, and depth-1 ctx::play_card/invoke_skill clear it. So the guard reads the ENCLOSING activation's origin, not this card's play, even though a counteract declaration is always a hand card. Countering a rent payment (outside any hand play, flag false) draws; countering a payment caused by a hand-played card (flag true) does not. Reproduced by rules/tests/tests/zz_ako_dark_probe.rs in this worktree: rent window counter from hand -> hand 4->4, draw 8->7, assertion 'played from hand: no draw' FAILED; card-payment window counter -> hand 3->2, draw pile 15->15.
- Fix: Arm an origin flag for the counteract declaration (hand vs field) in hand_counteractions/declare_one and expose it over the ABI, then gate the draw on that flag instead of the enclosing play's.

### `R:（燐子）Ringing Bloom` — extra_effect — `rules/cards/card-roselia/src/ringing_bloom.rs:119`
- Sheet: 「你的所有格子上的房屋数视为与你房屋数最多的格子等同，但受此效果影响获得额外房屋数的格子收费减半」
- Code: `    for &t in &raised_tiles(player_id, max_h) {         ctx::set_tile_prop(t, prop::RENT_FACTOR, 500);     }`
- Why: apply_view only SETS 500 on currently-raised tiles; it never clears RENT_FACTOR on a tile that has left the raised set. A raised tile built up to the max is no longer 「获得额外房屋数」 yet keeps charging half, and pay_after cannot repair it because it recomputes raised_tiles at pay time (a caught-up tile is excluded), so the factor survives the card's own discard. A raised tile sold to another player also keeps the half for a tile this effect never raised under the new owner. Neither TODO nor BACKLOG CRYSTAL-08 covers this (they name raze/sale staleness of the max and non-pay_after removal only).
- Fix: Clear RENT_FACTOR to 0 on every owned non-RiNG tile (and on discard) before re-setting 500 only on the recomputed raised set.

### `R:（燐子）Ringing Bloom` — wrong_behavior — `rules/cards/card-roselia/src/ringing_bloom.rs:120` [recorded]
- Sheet: 「（2）你的所有格子上的房屋数视为与你房屋数最多的格子等同，但受此效果影响获得额外房屋数的格子收费减半」
- Code: `ctx::set_tile_prop(t, prop::RENT_FACTOR, 500);`
- Why: apply_view only writes 500 onto tiles that are below the max *right now*; it never writes 0 for a tile that leaves the raised set. The engine build writes houses[t] first and only then raises houseAdded (play.rs:3059), so a raised tile built up to the max (or past it) is absent from raised_tiles at the very apply_view that should have disarmed it and keeps RENT_FACTOR=500 permanently. pay_after's disarm (line 155) iterates the same raised_tiles, so it misses the stale tile too, and the prop is on the board-owned tile instance, so a later owner outside this card also charges half. A tile at the max gains no extra houses and must charge in full.
- Fix: Reset RENT_FACTOR to 0 for all owned tiles first (or diff old vs new raised set), and clear it when a tile leaves the owner's set.

### `R:（纱夜）弹奏弹奏弹奏，继续弹奏` — wrong_behavior — `rules/cards/card-roselia/src/sayo_play.rs:22`
- Sheet: 「[反击] 时机合适时打出，打出时视为使用一次此卡使用者的技能。」
- Code: `"actor == owner && move.kind == Walk && move.main && slot('skill.sayoThorns.used') != turn_key",`
- Why: 「视为使用一次…技能」 runs the skill's contract: Sayo's (2) is 「一回合一次，你可以在进入移动阶段时消耗6个[火罐]将非[传送]的主要移动添加1或2格」 -- skill-personas/src/sayo.rs:47 puts `charge(owner) >= 6` in its pre and :121 spends 6. The card's pre has no charge term and the body (71-72) only sets the latch and steps, so it grants +1/+2 for free; sayo_card_timing.rs:76/91/113 assert charge stays 0 and :219-226 asserts the card is offered with 5 pots where the skill is not. The sibling 「视为使用」 card detour.rs:96-104 gates on the target skill's PRESS guard (which includes its charge) while its TODO:176-179 records paying as the open part -- this card does neither and has no TODO.
- Fix: Gate the pre on `charge(owner) >= 6` and spend 6 in the body (or invoke the skill body), with TODO(规则书) recording the open cost reading.

### `R:（纱夜）弹奏弹奏弹奏，继续弹奏` — wrong_behavior — `rules/cards/card-roselia/src/sayo_play.rs:72`
- Sheet: 「打出时视为使用一次此卡使用者的技能」
- Code: `    plan::set_steps(before + n);`
- Why: The sheet says playing the card counts as ONE USE of the user's skill. The bound skill is skill:冰川纱夜:踏上荆棘之路的觉悟 (2) 「一回合一次，你可以在进入移动阶段时消耗6个[火罐]将非[传送]的主要移动添加1或2格」 (rules/skills/skill-personas/src/sayo.rs:47): its use is gated on charge(owner) >= 6 and its body spends 6 (fire::spend at sayo.rs:121). The card body re-implements only the bump: no fire::spend(6) and no charge(owner) >= 6 in the pre (line 22). So the card is a free skill use, and sayo_card_timing.rs explicitly asserts charge is unchanged (lines 76, 91, 113) even from 0 fire. The repo's documented primitive for 「视为使用…技能」 is ctx::invoke_skill (ABI v35, crates/card-sdk/src/abi.rs:162-168), used that way by pareo_far.rs:57 and mutsumi_never.rs:90. No TODO and no BACKLOG item records the free extension, so it is unrecorded.
- Fix: Spend 6 火罐 and add charge(owner) >= 6 to the pre, or run ctx::invoke_skill(player_id, &skill) and drop the manual bump so the skill body pays.

### `Sumimi:#L11` — missing_clause — `rules/cards/card-sumimi/src/l11.rs:37`
- Sheet: 「每当乐队技能需要移除[奇迹水晶]时，可移除此卡上的一个[奇迹水晶]代替」
- Code: `// 「每当乐队技能需要移除[奇迹水晶]时，可移除此卡上的一个[奇迹水晶]代替」 --`
- Why: grep for `Sumimi:#L11` across rules/ returns only poppin.rs:300 and ave_mujica.rs:223. Every other band skill that removes its own crystals goes straight to `ctx::add_crystals(-n)`: pastel.rs:73 (-5), mygo.rs:157 (-1), mygo.rs:173 (-2), hhw.rs:98 (-1), sumimi.rs:89 (-all). Each is a 「乐队技能需要移除[奇迹水晶]」 case the clause covers, so five of six(seven) band removals ignore it. Confirmed.
- Fix: Extract one drain helper and call it at each band-skill crystal spend, not just poppin/ave_mujica.

### `Sumimi:#L11` — wrong_behavior — `rules/cards/card-sumimi/src/l11.rs:39`
- Sheet: 「每当乐队技能需要移除[奇迹水晶]时，可移除此卡上的一个[奇迹水晶]代替」
- Code: `// `poppin::cash`) drain `Sumimi:#L11` first, which is `CardL11.Cover(n)`.`
- Why: Tried to refute: the drain sites are the only two, and both sit behind a band-card-only cost gate — poppin `available` needs `ctx::crystals() >= 2` on the skill card (poppin.rs:217, reviewer said 220) and ave_mujica `can_halve` needs `ctx::crystals() >= 1` (ave_mujica.rs:213). With 2 crystals on L11 and 0 on the band card (0 stickers), `press`/`can_halve` refuse before `cash`/`halve` ever runs, so L11 can only reorder a payment the band card could already make, never stand in for it. `ctx::crystals()` is provably this card instance (bandori_std/src/lib.rs:195-197). Confirmed.
- Fix: Count L11 cover in the availability gates (and in the error-message choice), not only at the debit site.

### `Sumimi:（真奈）歌唱大赛5连冠` — missing_clause — `rules/cards/card-sumimi/src/mana_champion.rs:24`
- Sheet: 「[反击]当你或你的格子即将受到来自你以外的效果影响时打出此卡」
- Code: `"by != owner && by >= 0 && effect.hits(owner)",`
- Why: The pre guard only tests whether a declared effect names the owner via effect.hits; the 「或你的格子」 half has no test. effect.hits compiles to chain_hits (targets only), and tile-only effects that never name the tile's owner (house removal etc.) yield no hit, so the card is not offered at all. The same 「你或你拥有的格子」 wording in AG:宣战布告 adds an explicit effect::tile + tile_owner residual loop (declare_war.rs:61-65).
- Fix: Add the tile residual guard: any effect i with effect::tile(i) >= 0 && tile_owner(tile) == owner, like declare_war.rs:61-65.

### `Sumimi:（真奈）歌唱大赛5连冠` — wrong_behavior — `rules/cards/card-sumimi/src/mana_champion.rs:24`
- Sheet: 「当你或你的格子即将受到来自你以外的效果影响时打出此卡」
- Code: `"by != owner && by >= 0 && effect.hits(owner)",`
- Why: The CEL `effect.hits(x)` rewrites to `chain_hits` (crates/cond/src/schema.rs:225), and `chain_hits` maps only each effect's `target` (crates/game-rules/src/wasm_rules.rs:7971-7977). Two cases the clause names are missed: (a) a payment that makes you the payer -- `ctx::pay` declares `to = -1` (crates/game-rules/src/hostfns.rs:545-548), and the Pay trigger stores target=-1, from=payer (crates/game-core/src/engine/play.rs:3379-3385), so `effect.hits(owner)` is false for the payer; e.g. against another player's `通用:CiRCLE THANKS PARTY!` 「所有其他玩家可选择[消耗]500资金」 (thanks_party.rs:51-53) 真奈 is never offered, though the payment hits her; (b) an effect that touches a tile you own without naming you -- declared as `tile`, not `target`. The guest helper `ctx::effect::hits` counts `from` as well (crates/card-sdk/src/ctx.rs:3281-3292), and declare_war.rs:48-51 adds a `ctx::tile_owner` loop; the CEL path has neither.
- Fix: Gate with a guest `can_counteract` using `ctx::effect::hits(owner)` plus the tile-owner loop, as declare_war.rs does, instead of the target-only CEL predicate.

### `Sumimi:（真奈）歌唱大赛5连冠` — wrong_behavior — `rules/cards/card-sumimi/src/mana_champion.rs:24`
- Sheet: 「[反击]当你或你的格子即将受到来自你以外的效果影响时打出此卡，此时场上其他玩家可如同自身的对应目标被指定一般打出[反击]卡」
- Code: `        "by != owner && by >= 0 && effect.hits(owner)",`
- Why: Half-confirmed, mechanism corrected. `by` in the Effect pre is `t.by_card` (cond_pre.rs:1328; -1 = none) and `p.by_card` is copied into the effect window at play.rs:3377 — but `pay_rent` builds its Pay without `by_card` (play.rs:2439-2452), so a plain rent settlement declares with by = -1 and `by >= 0` closes the window. Rent does open the `effect` chain (it is a money movement, play.rs:3369-3387), so this is a live miss on the most common 「来自你以外的效果」. Empirically verified with a probe: landing on another player's 购物中心 charged rent (money moved) but the card was never offered, while the same hand was offered under 通用:登上武道馆. The second half of the original claim is wrong: `effect.hits(owner)` DOES catch tile hits — `target_tile` stamps the tile's owner as the effect target (wasm_rules.rs:5717, 5742-5749), so 「你的格子」 through the targeting pipeline is covered. Only untargeted tile writes (house removal and friends) still miss, the same residual gap 宣战布告 documents for its identical 「你拥有的格子」 wording. No TODO/BACKLOG item records any of this (card-sumimi row lists only CH-05, LINGER-10, MONEY-08, DONUT-VOID-01).
- Fix: Replace `by >= 0` with a source test that admits board rent without admitting your own board payments: `by != owner && (by >= 0 || pay_is_rent)` (dropping `by >= 0` outright would also offer the card on your own bank purchase, whose Pay has from = you and by = -1).

### `Sumimi:（真奈）歌唱大赛5连冠` — wrong_behavior — `rules/cards/card-sumimi/src/mana_champion.rs:28`
- Sheet: 「此时场上其他玩家可如同自身的对应目标被指定一般打出[反击]卡，且其反击卡中针对打出玩家自身的效果改为你。」
- Code: `.props(&[(prop::SELF_REDIRECT, 1)]).meta(&card_sdk::meta::CardMeta::new()`
- Why: The join/redirect is supposed to exist only while THIS card is played, but wasm_rules.rs:5963-5977 pre-scans every hand for any SELF_REDIRECT card and stamps `chain[0].link.join_redirect = Some(seat)` before any body runs, with no check that the card is ever declared and no clearing when it is not. A 真奈 merely sitting in a hand therefore arms the whole window: another player's [反击] with a self-targeted clause gets redirected to the holder and draws them. Probe: with 真奈 in P1's hand but never played, P2's 通用:网络链接异常 redirected its self-cancel and P2 drew (hand 1->1, deck 20->19); control without the card in hand: hand 1->0, deck 20->20. The pre-scan also takes the lowest seat with a copy, so with two copies the wrong holder can be armed even when the other one declares later.
- Fix: Arm `join_redirect` only when the SELF_REDIRECT counter is actually declared (clear the pre-scan value after the build round if the opener never lands).

### `(no card)` — wrong_behavior — `crates/game-core/src/engine/mod.rs:1316`
- Sheet: 「主要阶段中，若此卡上有至少9个水母标记，可以清除所有标记并传送到#4水族馆或者 #30弦卷豪宅，视为本次主要移动(喊出呼诶诶～!)，然后此卡置入弃牌堆」
- Code: `            if !id.starts_with("skill:") || !self.rules.has_play(&id) {`
- Why: The placed card's jump is only reachable through `act: "skill"` with the card id, but the viewer's skill list is restricted to `skill:*` instances. The web UI renders only that list (Side.tsx:218) and bot AI only scans persona/group skills (ai.rs:396), so after hand-placement no client control ever offers the jump. Verified: `view_extra_typed(0).skills` is `[]` with the card on the field.
- Fix: Include the viewer's public field instances whose rule declares an `On::Play` (the bodies branch on `is_placed`) in the pressable list, or add a field-card press surface.

### `通用:10次招募（1回限定）` — wrong_behavior — `rules/cards/card-general/src/gacha10.rs:40`
- Sheet: 「[消耗]1500资金，抽1张卡。」
- Code: `if paid > 0 {`
- Why: Confirmed. money_inner returns Paid::default() (moved()=0) whenever no money moves: money_locked (play.rs:3301-3310), an `effect`/`paySplitChoose`/`pay` cancel (3369-3399, 3530-3557), a final amount of 0 after modifiers (3659-3668), or bankruptcy after raise_funds (3730-3734). In every one of those the payment step still resolved and the book's second clause 「抽1张卡」 still stands, but the card skips it; `ctx::draw` is not money, so not even a 「无法获得或失去资金」 lock excuses it. The sheet has two unconditional clauses and the Kani contract (rules/kani/src/spec/general.rs:36-40) models success as exactly PayMoney(1500)+Draw(1); the money::gacha10_playable_while_short_then_bankrupts test (money.rs:417-432) only asserts bankruptcy, never the draw, so nothing catches this.
- Fix: Draw unconditionally after the payment (ctx::draw already returns 0 for an out seat), or record the gate with a TODO(规则书).

### `通用:网络链接异常` — wrong_behavior — `rules/cards/card-general/src/net_error.rs:120` [recorded]
- Sheet: 「手卡的[手]效果且有[指定]目标则取消其对目标之一的[指定]；」
- Code: `                    let seat = self_seat.unwrap_or(designations[0]);`
- Why: ctx::designations is the static DESIGNATES list and includes seats already cancelled. A seat keeps its visit floor and is re-offered after each declaration (wasm_rules.rs:6018-6049), and this card carries no COUNTERACT_GROUP, so a second copy played in the same window re-picks the same self seat; ctx::cancel_designation is a no-op for a seat already in cancelled_designations (wasm_rules.rs:2105-2108). The second card is spent while another target's designation still lands, contrary to 「取消其对目标之一的[指定]」.
- Fix: Filter designations with `!ctx::designation_cancelled(s)` before choosing, and do not offer when none remain.

### `通用:网络链接异常` — wrong_behavior — `rules/cards/card-general/src/net_error.rs:148` [recorded]
- Sheet: 「1. 手卡的[手]效果且有[指定]目标则取消其对目标之一的[指定]；」
- Code: `        _ => {}`
- Why: The entry registers ChainKind::Effect (line 21) and can_counteract answers TriggerKind::Effect (lines 60-65); abi.rs:2027-2035 documents `effect` as "the counteraction key for being hit by another player's effect". But the counteracts match has only Event / Card / Target arms, so an `effect` trigger falls to `_ => {}`: no set_cancelled, no cancel_designation, no log, and the card is still consumed (dest_from(-1) is Graveyard). target_window raises `effect` at wasm_rules.rs:5742 for every ctx::target declaration, so the card is offered and played for nothing.
- Fix: Add a TriggerKind::Effect arm that set_cancelled (not just cancel_designation — see verify note) and logs clause 1, and TODO(规则书) any deferred narrowing.

Edge-case wrongness, unimplemented clauses and dangerous comment drift. One line each.
